## Customs Tariff {#customs-tariff .Title-of-Act}

[S.C.]{.abbr title="Statutes of Canada"} 1997, c. 36
:::

Assented to 1997-12-08

An Act respecting the imposition of duties of customs and other charges,
to give effect to the International Convention on the Harmonized
Commodity Description and Coding System, to provide relief against the
imposition of certain duties of customs or other charges, to provide for
other related matters and to amend or repeal certain Acts in consequence
thereof
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: Enacts
Her Majesty, by and with the advice and consent of the Senate and House
of Commons of Canada, enacts as follows:
:::

## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-142680 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011).

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Interpretation and General]{.HTitleText1} {#h-142683 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-142684 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  definitions in this subsection apply in this Act.

  [[Canada--Chile Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--Chile Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      same meaning as [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection
      2(1) of the Canada--Chile Free Trade Agreement Implementation
      Act. ([Accord de libre-échange Canada --- Chili]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Canada--Colombia Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--Colombia Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the same meaning as [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section
      2 of the [Canada--Colombia Free Trade Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-1.65). ([Accord de libre-échange
      Canada--Colombie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada --- Costa Rica Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada --- Costa Rica Free Trade
      Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 2(1) of the
      Canada --- Costa Rica Free Trade Agreement Implementation
      Act; ([Accord de libre-échange Canada --- Costa
      Rica]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada--EFTA Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--EFTA Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      same meaning as [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection
      2(1) of the Canada--EFTA Agreement Implementation Act. ([Accord de
      libre-échange Canada--AELÉ]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada--European Union Comprehensive Economic and Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--European Union Comprehensive Economic and Trade
      Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--European Union Comprehensive Economic and Trade Agreement
      Implementation Act](/eng/acts/C-4.8). ([Accord économique et
      commercial global entre le Canada et l'Union
      européenne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada--Honduras Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--Honduras Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the same meaning as [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section
      2 of the [Canada--Honduras Economic Growth and Prosperity
      Act](/eng/acts/C-6.10). ([Accord de libre-échange
      Canada-Honduras]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada--Israel Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--Israel Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the same meaning as [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in
      subsection 2(1) of the Canada--Israel Free Trade Agreement
      Implementation Act. ([Accord de libre-échange Canada ---
      Israël]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada--Jordan Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--Jordan Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the same meaning as [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section
      2 of the [Canada--Jordan Economic Growth and Prosperity
      Act](/eng/acts/C-6.45). ([Accord de libre-échange
      Canada-Jordanie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada--Korea Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--Korea Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      same meaning as [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2
      of the [Canada--Korea Economic Growth and Prosperity
      Act](/eng/acts/C-6.48). ([Accord de libre-échange
      Canada-Corée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada--Panama Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--Panama Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the same meaning as [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section
      2 of the [Canada--Panama Economic Growth and Prosperity
      Act](/eng/acts/C-7.95). ([Accord de libre-échange
      Canada-Panama]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada--Peru Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--Peru Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      same meaning as [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2
      of the [Canada--Peru Free Trade Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-8.4). ([Accord de libre-échange
      Canada-Pérou]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada--Ukraine Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--Ukraine Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the meaning assigned by the definition
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--Ukraine Free Trade Agreement Implementation Act,
      2023](/eng/acts/C-10.541). ([Accord de libre-échange
      Canada--Ukraine]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada--United Kingdom Trade Continuity Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--United Kingdom Trade Continuity
      Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by the
      definition [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--United Kingdom Trade Continuity Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-10.56). ([Accord de continuité commerciale du
      Canada--Royaume-Uni]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada--United States Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--United States Free Trade
      Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s.
      183\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Canada--United States--Mexico Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada--United States--Mexico
      Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by the
      definition [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--United States--Mexico Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-10.65). ([Accord
      Canada--États-Unis--Mexique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Chile]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Chile]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the land, maritime, and air
      space under the sovereignty of Chile and the exclusive economic
      zone and the continental shelf over which it exercises sovereign
      rights and jurisdiction in accordance with its domestic law and
      international law. ([Chili]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Colombia]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Colombia]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the land territory, both
      continental and insular, the air space and the maritime areas over
      which the Republic of Colombia exercises sovereignty, or sovereign
      rights or jurisdiction, in accordance with its domestic law and
      international law. ([Colombie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Comprehensive and Progressive Agreement for Trans-Pacific Partnership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Comprehensive and Progressive Agreement for Trans-Pacific
      Partnership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by the
      definition [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Comprehensive and Progressive Agreement for Trans-Pacific
      Partnership Implementation Act](/eng/acts/C-36.45). ([Accord de
      partenariat transpacifique global et
      progressiste]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Costa Rica]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Costa Rica]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the territory and air
      space, and the maritime areas, including the seabed and subsoil
      adjacent to the outer limit of the territorial sea, over which it
      exercises, in accordance with international law and its domestic
      law, sovereign rights with respect to the natural resources of
      such areas; ([Costa Rica]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, unless the context otherwise
      requires, includes an external or dependent territory of a country
      and any other prescribed territory. ([pays]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[CPTPP country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CPTPP country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a party to the
      Comprehensive and Progressive Agreement for Trans-Pacific
      Partnership. ([pays PTPGP]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CPTPP tariff]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CPTPP tariff]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any of the following
      tariff treatments:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific
        Partnership Tariff;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Comprehensive and Progressive Australia
        Tariff;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Comprehensive and Progressive Brunei
        Tariff;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the Comprehensive and Progressive Chile Tariff;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the Comprehensive and Progressive Japan Tariff;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the Comprehensive and Progressive Malaysia
        Tariff;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the Comprehensive and Progressive Mexico
        Tariff;

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the Comprehensive and Progressive New Zealand
        Tariff;

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the Comprehensive and Progressive Peru Tariff;

      - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the Comprehensive and Progressive Singapore
        Tariff; and

      - [(k)]{.lawlabel} the Comprehensive and Progressive Vietnam
        Tariff. ([tarif PTPGP]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CUKTCA beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CUKTCA beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the United
      Kingdom, including the Channel Islands, Gibraltar and the Isle of
      Man, but excluding Anguilla, the British Antarctic Territory, the
      British Indian Ocean Territory, Bermuda, the British Virgin
      Islands, the Cayman Islands, the Falkland Islands, Montserrat,
      Pitcairn, Saint Helena and Dependencies (Ascension Island and
      Tristan Da Cunha), South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands
      and the Turks and Caicos Islands. ([bénéficiaire de
      l'ACCCRU]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[customs duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[customs duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, except for the purposes of
      Part 3, other than sections 82 and 122, means a duty imposed under
      section 20. ([droits de douane]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EU country or other CETA beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EU country or other CETA beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the meaning assigned by regulation. ([pays de l'Union européenne
      ou autre bénéficiaire de l'AÉCG]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[final rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[final rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the rate of customs duty
      that applies after the application of all reductions provided for
      under this Act, other than reductions related to rounding or to
      the elimination of rates of less than two per cent. ([taux
      final]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[for use in]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[for use in]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, wherever it appears in a
      tariff item, in respect of goods classified in the tariff item,
      means that the goods must be wrought or incorporated into, or
      attached to, other goods referred to in that tariff item. ([devant
      servir dans]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [devant servir
      à]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[free trade partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[free trade partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the United States;

      - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} Mexico;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Chile; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Israel or another CIFTA
        beneficiary. ([partenaire de libre-échange]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [["F" Staging List]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [["F" Staging List]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the List of
      Intermediate and Final Rates for Tariff Items of the "F" Staging
      Category set out in the schedule. ([tableau des
      échelonnements]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[heading]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[heading]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of goods, means a
      description in the List of Tariff Provisions accompanied by a
      four-digit number and includes all subheadings or tariff items the
      first four digits of which correspond to that
      number. ([position]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Honduras]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Honduras]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the land, maritime areas,
      and air space under the sovereignty of the Republic of Honduras
      and the exclusive economic zone and the continental shelf within
      which it exercises sovereign rights and jurisdiction in accordance
      with its domestic law and international
      law. ([Honduras]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Iceland]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Iceland]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the land territory, air space, internal waters
        and territorial sea of Iceland; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the exclusive economic zone and continental
        shelf of Iceland. ([Islande]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[imported from Israel or another CIFTA beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[imported from Israel or another CIFTA
      beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by
      regulation. ([importé d'Israël ou d'un autre bénéficiaire de
      l'ALÉCI]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[initial rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[initial rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the rate of customs
      duty that applies before any reductions are made under this
      Act. ([taux initial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Israel or another CIFTA beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Israel or another CIFTA beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the meaning assigned by regulation. ([Israël ou autre bénéficiaire
      de l'ALÉCI]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Jordan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Jordan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the land territory, the air
      space and the internal waters and territorial sea over which the
      Hashemite Kingdom of Jordan exercises
      sovereignty. ([Jordanie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Korea]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Korea]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the land, maritime areas and
      air space under the sovereignty of the Republic of Korea, and
      those maritime areas, including the seabed and subsoil adjacent to
      and beyond the outer limit of the territorial seas, over which the
      Republic of Korea may exercise sovereign rights or jurisdiction in
      accordance with international law and its domestic
      law. ([Corée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Liechtenstein]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Liechtenstein]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the land territory and
      air space of Liechtenstein. ([Liechtenstein]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[List of Countries]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[List of Countries]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the List of
      Countries and Applicable Tariff Treatments set out in the
      schedule. ([tableau des traitements tarifaires]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[List of Tariff Provisions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[List of Tariff Provisions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the List
      of Tariff Provisions set out in the schedule. ([liste des
      dispositions tarifaires]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Mexico]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Mexico]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by
      paragraph (b) of the definition
      [[territory]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in Section C of Chapter 1 of
      the Canada--United States--Mexico
      Agreement. ([Mexique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of
      Finance. ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[NAFTA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[NAFTA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s.
      183\]]{.Repealed}

  [[North American Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[North American Free Trade
      Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s.
      183\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Norway]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Norway]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the land territory, air space, internal waters
        and territorial sea of Norway; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the exclusive economic zone and continental
        shelf of Norway. ([Norvège]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Panama]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Panama]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the land, maritime areas, and
      air space under the sovereignty of the Republic of Panama and the
      exclusive economic zone and the continental shelf within which it
      exercises sovereign rights and jurisdiction in accordance with its
      domestic law and international law. ([Panama]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Peru]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Peru]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the mainland territory, the
      islands, the maritime zones and the air space above them, over
      which the Republic of Peru exercises sovereignty, or sovereign
      rights and jurisdiction, in accordance with its domestic law and
      international law. ([Pérou]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a form, the information to be
        provided on or with a form, or the manner of filing a form,
        prescribed by the Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
        Preparedness; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, prescribed by regulation or
        determined in accordance with rules prescribed by
        regulation. ([Version anglaise
        seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

  [[regulation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[regulation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a regulation made under
      this Act. ([règlement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[serious injury]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[serious injury]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to
      domestic producers of like or directly competitive goods, a
      significant overall impairment in the position of the domestic
      producers. ([dommage grave]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[specific rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specific rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a rate expressed in
      dollars or cents per unit of measure. ([taux
      spécifique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[specified rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specified rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the rate of interest,
      expressed as a percentage per year, equal to six per cent per year
      plus the prescribed rate. ([taux déterminé]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[subheading]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[subheading]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of goods, means a
      description in the List of Tariff Provisions accompanied by a
      six-digit number and includes all tariff items the first six
      digits of which correspond to that
      number. ([sous-position]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Switzerland]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Switzerland]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the land territory and
      air space of Switzerland. ([Suisse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tariff item]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tariff item]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a description of goods
      in the List of Tariff Provisions and the rates of customs duty and
      the accompanying eight-digit number in that List and, if
      applicable, in the "F" Staging List. ([numéro
      tarifaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tariff rate quota]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tariff rate quota]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a limitation on
      the quantity of goods that are entitled to a specified tariff
      treatment that may be imported in a specified period. ([contingent
      tarifaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[threat of serious injury]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[threat of serious injury]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means serious
      injury that, on the basis of facts, and not merely of allegation,
      conjecture or remote possibility, is clearly imminent. ([menace de
      dommage grave]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Ukraine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Ukraine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the land territory, air space, internal waters
        and territorial sea of Ukraine;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the exclusive economic zone of Ukraine; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the continental shelf of
        Ukraine. ([Ukraine]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[UNCLOS]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[UNCLOS]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2024, c. 3, s.
      34\]]{.Repealed}

  [[United States]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[United States]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the customs territory of the United States,
        including the 50 states of the United States, the District of
        Columbia and Puerto Rico;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the foreign trade zones located in the United
        States and Puerto Rico; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any areas beyond the territorial sea of the
        United States within which the United States may exercise rights
        in respect of the seabed and subsoil and the natural resources
        thereof, in accordance with its domestic laws and international
        law. ([États-Unis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[World Trade Organization Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[World Trade Organization Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      same meaning as [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection
      2(1) of the [World Trade Organization Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/W-11.8). ([Accord sur l'Organisation mondiale du
      commerce]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Territories

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A territory prescribed by regulation for the purposes
  of the definition [[country]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection (1)
  is not, by virtue of being so prescribed, recognized as a country for
  purposes other than this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elements of the List of Tariff Provisions

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** The List of Tariff
Provisions is divided into Sections, Chapters and sub-Chapters.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Words and expressions in Act

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** Unless otherwise provided,
words and expressions used in this Act and defined in subsection 2(1) of
the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) have the same meaning as in that
subsection.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Goods imported from certain countries

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Act, goods are imported from one of the following countries if they are
shipped directly to Canada from that country:

- ::::: listItemBlock0

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Percentage rates

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Act, if a rate of customs duty is expressed as a percentage or includes
a percentage, the customs duties imposed shall be calculated in
accordance with section 44 of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Weight of goods

**[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Act, if a rate of customs duty is based in whole or in part on the
weight of goods, the customs duties imposed on the goods are, unless
otherwise provided, to be calculated on the basis of the net weight of
the goods.

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-142807 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction of Canadian waters

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty, a
regulation made under subsection 2(2) of the [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) applies so as to temporarily restrict, for the
purposes of this Act, the extent of Canadian waters, including the
inland waters.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation of powers

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** The Minister of Public
Safety and Emergency Preparedness may authorize an officer or agent or a
class of officers or agents to exercise powers or perform duties of that
Minister under this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 9
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-9-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 9"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classification of goods in the List of
Tariff Provisions

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  classification of imported goods under a tariff item shall, unless
  otherwise provided, be determined in accordance with the General Rules
  for the Interpretation of the Harmonized System and the Canadian Rules
  set out in the schedule.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classification of "within access
  commitment" goods

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Goods shall not be classified under a tariff item
  that contains the phrase "within access commitment" unless the goods
  are imported under the authority of a permit issued under section 8.3
  of the [Export and Import Permits Act](/eng/acts/E-19) and in
  compliance with the conditions of the permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** In interpreting the
headings and subheadings, regard shall be had to the Compendium of
Classification Opinions to the Harmonized Commodity Description and
Coding System and the Explanatory Notes to the Harmonized Commodity
Description and Coding System, published by the Customs Co-operation
Council (also known as the World Customs Organization), as amended from
time to time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administration and enforcement

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** The provisions of the
[Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) apply, with such modifications as the
circumstances require, in respect of the administration and enforcement
of this Act and the regulations, and for the purposes thereof, a
contravention of this Act or the regulations or a failure to comply with
a condition to which relief or a remission, drawback or refund under
Part 3 is subject or to which classification under a tariff item is
subject is deemed to be a contravention of the [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

### [Amendment of Schedule]{.HTitleText2} {#h-142823 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment --- List of Tariff Provisions

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, by
order, amend the List of Tariff Provisions to change a tariff item
number or a description of goods in a tariff item, or to add, repeal or
replace a tariff item, if the amendment does not affect the rate of
customs duty applicable to those goods.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 13
- 2011, c. 24, s. 112
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 13"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment of List of Tariff Provisions

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, by
  order, amend the schedule, other than tariff item No. 9898.00.00 or
  9899.00.00, to give effect to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an amendment to the Harmonized Commodity
    Description and Coding System or to any advice on the interpretation
    of that System approved by the Customs Co-operation Council (also
    known as the World Customs Organization);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a modification to an agreement or arrangement
    relating to international trade to which the Government of Canada is
    a party; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an arrangement or commitment that extends the
    benefits of an agreement relating to international trade to which
    the Government of Canada is a party.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reciprocal reductions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of
  the Minister, by order, amend the schedule to reduce a rate of customs
  duty on goods imported from a country, and to make consequential
  amendments,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by way of compensation for concessions granted by
    the country or any other country, subject to any conditions set out
    in the order;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as may be required by Canada's international
    obligations, subject to any conditions set out in the order; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} by way of compensation for any action taken under
    any of the following provisions:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsection 55(1),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} section 60,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} subsection 63(1),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s. 185\]]{.Repealed}

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s. 185\]]{.Repealed}

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} subsection 71(2),

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} subsection 71.01(1),

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} subsection 71.1(2),

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} subsection 71.41(1),

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} subsection 71.5(1),

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} subsection 71.6(1),

    - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} subsection 72(2),

    - [(xiii)]{.lawlabel} subsection 73(1),

    - [(xiv)]{.lawlabel} subsection 74(1),

    - [(xv)]{.lawlabel} subsection 74(2),

    - [(xvi)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2024, c. 3, s. 35\]]{.Repealed}

    - [(xvii)]{.lawlabel} subsection 76(1),

    - [(xviii)]{.lawlabel} subsection 77(1),

    - [(xix)]{.lawlabel} subsection 5(3), (3.2) or (4.1) of the [Export
      and Import Permits Act](/eng/acts/E-19).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order may be retroactive

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (2) may, if it so
  provides, be retroactive and have effect in respect of a period before
  it is made that begins after the coming into force of this section.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}List of Countries

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may, by order, amend the List of Countries to reflect a
  change in the name of a country set out in that List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An amendment under subsection (1) does not affect the
  tariff treatments indicated for the country in respect of which the
  change is made.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 15
- 2011, c. 24, s. 114

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Customs Duties]{.HTitleText1} {#h-142869 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Origin of Goods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-142870 .Subheading}

#### [Rules of Origin]{.HTitleText3} {#h-142871 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of
[[originate]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any regulations made
  under subsection (2), for the purposes of this Act, goods originate in
  a country if the whole of the value of the goods is produced in that
  country.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rules of origin regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of
  the Minister, make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the origin of goods, including
    regulations

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} deeming goods, the whole or a portion of which is
      produced outside a country, to originate in that country for the
      purposes of this Act or any other Act of Parliament, subject to
      such conditions as are specified in the regulations,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} deeming goods, the whole or a portion of which
      is produced within a geographic area of a country, not to
      originate in that country for the purposes of this Act or any
      other Act of Parliament and not to be entitled to the preferential
      tariff treatment otherwise applicable under this Act, subject to
      such conditions as are specified in the regulations, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for determining when goods originate in a
      country for the purposes of this Act or any other Act of
      Parliament; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for determining when goods are entitled to a tariff
    treatment under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[geographic
  area]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} In subsections (2) and 49.1(4), [[geographic
  area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any area specified by the Minister of
  Public Safety and Emergency Preparedness after consultation with the
  Minister for International Trade.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of rules of origin
  regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of implementing the Agreement on
  Rules of Origin in Annex 1A of the World Trade Organization Agreement
  and any annex added to it under Article 9 of that Agreement,
  regulations made under subsection (2) may, if they so provide, prevail
  over any other regulations to the extent of any inconsistency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Uniform regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of
  the Minister, make regulations for the uniform interpretation,
  application and administration of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Chapters 4 and 6 of the Canada--United
    States--Mexico Agreement and any other matters agreed on from time
    to time by the parties to that Agreement for the purposes of that
    Agreement;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Chapters C and D of the Canada--Chile Free Trade
    Agreement and any other matters agreed on from time to time by the
    parties to that Agreement for the purposes of that Agreement; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Chapters III and IV of the Canada --- Costa Rica
    Free Trade Agreement and any other matters agreed on from time to
    time by the parties to that Agreement for the purposes of that
    Agreement.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 16
- 2001, c. 28, s. 34
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
- [2020, c. 1, s. 186](#1238295-1253337){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1238295-1253337.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-16-20051212.html "Link to previous version of section 16"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Direct Shipment]{.HTitleText3} {#h-142893 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shipment to Canada

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Act, goods are shipped directly to Canada from
  another country when the goods are shipped to Canada from that other
  country in accordance with the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of determining whether goods are
  shipped directly to Canada, the Governor in Council may, on the
  recommendation of the Minister, make regulations respecting the
  shipping of goods.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 17
- [2023, c. 26, s. 229](#1409810-1506779){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1409810-1506779.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-17-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 17"){.PITLink}
:::

s. 229\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-18-20051212.html "Link to previous version of section 18"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Marking of Goods]{.HTitleText3} {#h-142910 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations requiring marking

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, make
  regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} requiring imported goods of any description or
    class, including a description or class specified in terms of the
    use of the goods, to be marked, in accordance with regulations made
    under subsection (2), so as to indicate their country or geographic
    area of origin; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for determining the country or geographic area of
    origin of imported goods for marking purposes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations prescribing marking
  requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
  Preparedness may make regulations for the purpose of the
  administration of this section, including regulations prescribing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which imported goods must be marked
    and any conditions applicable to the marking of the goods; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} when imported goods must be marked, including
    whether they must be marked before or after importation, and any
    conditions applicable to the time of marking.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicability of regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Regulations made under this section may apply
  generally or be limited to particular countries or geographic areas
  defined in the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 19
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Imposition of Customs Duties]{.HTitleText2} {#h-142924 .Subheading}

#### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#h-142925 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imposition of customs duty

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless
  otherwise indicated in Chapter 98 or 99 of the List of Tariff
  Provisions, in addition to any other duties imposed under this Act or
  any other Act of Parliament relating to customs, there shall be levied
  on all goods set out in the List of Tariff Provisions, at the time
  those goods are imported, and paid in accordance with the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), a customs duty at the rates set out in that
  List, the "F" Staging List or section 29 that are applicable to those
  goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Value for duty of Canadian goods
  returned

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 44 of the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), the value for duty of goods that have been
  taken out of Canada and are subsequently returned to Canada is the
  value of the goods at the time of the subsequent return if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the goods were repaired outside Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} equipment was added to the goods outside Canada; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} work was done outside Canada on the goods.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in sections 21.1 to 21.3.

[[beer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[beer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[malt
    liquor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means beer or malt liquor, within the
    meaning of section 4 of the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14), of tariff
    item No. 2202.91.00 or heading 22.03, that is classified under that
    heading or tariff item or with the container in which it is
    imported. ([bière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [liqueur de
    malt]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bulk]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bulk]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section 2 of
    the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1). ([en
    vrac]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[excise warehouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[excise warehouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    section 2 of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1). ([entrepôt
    d'accise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[excise warehouse licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[excise warehouse licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
    meaning as in section 2 of the [Excise Act,
    2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1). ([exploitant agréé d'entrepôt
    d'accise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[licensed user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[licensed user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    section 2 of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1). ([utilisateur
    agréé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[packaged]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[packaged]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section
    2 of the [Excise Act,
    2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1). ([emballé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[specified premises]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[specified premises]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    in section 2 of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1). ([local
    déterminé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[spirits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[spirits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means spirits, as defined in section
    2 of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1),

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} of an alcoholic strength by volume exceeding
      22.9%, of tariff item No. 2204.10.90, 2204.21.32, 2204.21.49,
      2204.22.32, 2204.22.49, 2204.29.32, 2204.29.49, 2204.30.90,
      2205.10.30, 2205.90.30, 2206.00.19, 2206.00.22, 2206.00.39,
      2206.00.49, 2206.00.72 or 2206.00.93, that are classified under
      that tariff item or with the container in which they are imported;

    - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} of an alcoholic strength by volume exceeding
      11.9%, of heading No. 22.03, that are classified under that
      heading or with the container in which they are imported; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} of heading No. 22.07 or 22.08, other than of
      tariff item No. 2207.20.11, 2207.20.12, 2207.20.90 or 2208.90.30,
      that are classified under that heading or with the container in
      which they are imported. ([spiritueux]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[wine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[wine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means wine, as defined in section 2 of
    the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), of heading 22.04, 22.05 or
    22.06, other than of tariff item No. 2204.10.90, 2204.21.32,
    2204.21.49, 2204.22.32, 2204.22.49, 2204.29.32, 2204.29.49,
    2204.30.90, 2205.10.30, 2205.90.30, 2206.00.19, 2206.00.22,
    2206.00.39, 2206.00.49, 2206.00.72 or 2206.00.93, that is classified
    under that heading or with the container in which it is
    imported. ([vin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 21

- 2002, c. 22, ss. 346, 412
- 2003, c. 15, s. 45
- 2007, c. 18, s. 142
- 2008, c. 28, s. 70
- 2018, c. 27, s. 69
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-21-20080618.html "Link to previous version of section 21"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional duty on bulk spirits

- **[[21.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  addition to any other duties imposed under this Act or any other Act
  of Parliament relating to customs, there is levied on bulk spirits, at
  the time they are imported, an additional duty equal to the duty that
  would be imposed on the spirits under section 122 of the [Excise Act,
  2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) if the spirits had been produced in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty payable under [Excise Act,
  2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The duty levied on bulk spirits shall be paid and
  collected under the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), and interest
  and penalties shall be imposed, calculated, paid and collected under
  that Act, as if the duty were duty imposed on the spirits under that
  Act, and, for those purposes, that Act applies with any modifications
  that the circumstances require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2) and the [Excise Act,
  2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), the person who is liable for duty imposed
  under subsection (1) in respect of bulk spirits that have not been
  released under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) is the person who
  is liable to pay duties under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 346
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional duty on packaged spirits

- **[[21.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  addition to any other duties imposed under this Act or any other Act
  of Parliament relating to customs, there is levied on packaged
  spirits, at the time they are imported, and paid in accordance with
  the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), an additional duty equal to the
  duty that would be imposed on them under section 122 or 123 of the
  [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) if they had been produced and
  packaged in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional duty on packaged wine

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any other duties imposed under this
  Act or any other Act of Parliament relating to customs, there is
  levied on packaged wine, at the time it is imported, and paid in
  accordance with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), an additional
  duty equal to the duty that would be imposed on it under section 135
  of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) if it had been packaged in
  Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Goods entered into warehouse or
  premises

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, immediately after being released under the
  [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), packaged spirits or wine is entered
  into the excise warehouse of the excise warehouse licensee or the
  specified premises of the licensed user who imported the spirits or
  wine, the duty levied on the spirits or wine under subsection (1)
  or (2) shall be paid and collected under the [Excise Act,
  2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1). Interest and penalties shall be imposed,
  calculated, paid and collected under the [Excise Act,
  2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) as if the duty were imposed under that Act,
  and, for those purposes, that Act applies with any modifications that
  the circumstances require.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 346
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional duty on beer

**[[21.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21.3 .sectionLabel}** In addition to any
other duties imposed under this Act or any other Act of Parliament
relating to customs, there is levied on beer or malt liquor, at the time
it is imported, and paid in accordance with the [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), an additional duty equal to the duty that would
be levied on it under section 170 of the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14) if
it had been manufactured or produced in Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 22, s. 346
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other duties

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** In addition to the duties
imposed under this Act or any other Act of Parliament relating to
customs, there shall be levied on imported goods, at the time of their
importation, and paid in accordance with the [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), a duty comprised of any surtax or temporary duty
imposed under Division 4 of this Part.

#### [Special Classification]{.HTitleText3} {#h-142974 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Goods of Chapter 99

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** Goods of Chapter 99 of
the List of Tariff Provisions are entitled to the rate of customs duty
set out for those goods in the column entitled "Most-Favoured-Nation
Tariff" or "Preferential Tariff" in that Chapter, according to the
tariff treatment applicable to their country of origin.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Tariff Treatments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-142977 .Subheading}

#### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#h-142978 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless
  otherwise provided in an order made under subsection (2) or otherwise
  specified in a tariff item, goods are entitled to a tariff treatment,
  other than the General Tariff, under this Act only if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} proof of origin of the goods is given in accordance
    with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the goods are entitled to that tariff treatment in
    accordance with regulations made under section 16 or an order made
    under any of the following provisions:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 31(1)(a),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 34(1)(a),

    - [(ii.1)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 36.2(1)(a),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 38(1)(a),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 42(1)(a),

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} subsection 45(7),

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} section 48,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} subsection 49.01(8),

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} section 49.2,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} subsection 49.5(8),

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} subsection 49.6(8).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of
  the Minister, by order, exempt goods entitled to a tariff treatment
  other than the General Tariff from any condition set out in subsection
  (1), on such conditions as may be specified in the order.

::: HistoricalNote

Version](section-24-20200701.html "Link to previous version of section 24"){.PITLink}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Most favourable tariff

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** If, under this Act, goods
are entitled to both the Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff and another Tariff
and the amount of customs duty imposed under the Most-Favoured-Nation
Tariff is lower than the amount imposed under the other Tariff, the rate
of customs duty under the Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff applies to those
goods in lieu of the rate under the other Tariff.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Goods in transit

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** An order made under
paragraph 31(1)(b), 34(1)(b), 38(1)(b) or 42(1)(b) may provide that
goods that are in transit to Canada at the time the order comes into
force are entitled to the tariff treatment that was applicable to those
goods immediately before that time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If rate not specified

**[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** The symbol "N/A", if it
is set out in the column entitled "Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff" in the
List of Tariff Provisions, or in the column entitled "Preferential
Tariff" in that List in combination with an abbreviation designating a
preferential tariff treatment of a tariff item, indicates that that
tariff treatment does not apply to that tariff item.

#### [General Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143042 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of General Tariff

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  General Tariff rate of customs duty of 35% applies to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} goods that originate in a country that is not set
    out in the List of Countries;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} goods that originate in a country set out in the
    List of Countries and that fail to meet the conditions for
    entitlement to any other tariff treatments provided for under this
    Act; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} goods to which the General Tariff applies under
    paragraph 31(1)(b) or any regulation or order made under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), goods referred to in
  that subsection are subject to the Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff rate of
  customs duty in respect of those goods if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that rate is, or is equivalent to, more than 35%;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a Note or Supplementary Note to a Chapter of the
    List of Tariff Provisions or a tariff item so provides.

#### [Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143053 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of MFN Tariff

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24 and any order
  made under section 31, goods that originate in a country set out in
  the List of Countries are entitled to the Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff
  rates of customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions in
  relation to goods entitled to the Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff, the
  Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those
  goods is the final rate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staging for MFN Tariff

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "B", "C", "D" or "E" is set out in the column
  entitled "Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff" in the List of Tariff
  Provisions in relation to goods entitled to the Most-Favoured-Nation
  Tariff, the Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff rate of customs duty that
  applies to those goods is the initial rate, reduced

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if "B" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 1999, by one half of the
      difference between the initial rate and the final rate, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2000, to the final rate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if "C" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on August 1, 1998, by one third of the
      difference between the initial rate and the final rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on August 1, 1999, by two thirds of
      the difference between the initial rate and the final rate, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on August 1, 2000, to the final rate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if "D" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 1999, by one quarter of
      the difference between the initial rate and the final rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2000, by one half of the
      difference between the initial rate and the final rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2001, by three quarters
      of the difference between the initial rate and the final rate, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2002, to the final rate;
      and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if "E" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 1999, by one sixth of the
      difference between the initial rate and the final rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2000, by one third of
      the difference between the initial rate and the final rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2001, by one half of
      the difference between the initial rate and the final rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2002, by two thirds of
      the difference between the initial rate and the final rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2003, by five sixths of
      the difference between the initial rate and the final rate, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2004, to the final rate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for MFN Tariff

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions in
  relation to goods entitled to the Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff, the
  Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those
  goods is the initial rate, reduced as provided in the "F" Staging
  List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"G" staging for MFN Tariff

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If "G" is set out in the column entitled
  "Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions in
  relation to goods entitled to the Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff, the
  Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those
  goods is the initial rate, reduced, effective January 1, 1999, to the
  final rate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding percentage rates

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3), (4) or (5)
  results in a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per
  cent, the resulting percentage shall be rounded to the nearest
  one-tenth of one per cent or, if the resulting percentage is
  equidistant from two one-tenths of one percent, to the higher of them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of rates other than 0.5 per
  cent

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If, for any goods other than motor vehicles of tariff
  item No. 8701.20.00, heading No. 87.02, 87.03, 87.04 or 87.05, and
  chassis therefor of heading No. 87.06, a reduction under any of
  subsections (3) to (5) or a rounding of rates under subsection (6)
  results in a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per
  cent other than 0.5, the resulting percentage shall be rounded down to
  the nearest percentage that divides evenly by 0.5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elimination of rates of less than two
  per cent

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3), (4) or (5)
  results in a rate of customs duty that is a percentage of less than
  two per cent, the rate shall be further reduced to "Free" immediately.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding specific rates

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3), (4) or (5)
  results in a rate of customs duty that includes a specific rate that
  includes a fraction of one cent and the final rate

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is or includes a specific rate, the specific rate
    component of the reduced rate shall be rounded

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the final rate is or includes a specific rate
      expressed in cents to two decimal places, to the nearest
      one-hundredth of a cent or, if the specific rate component of the
      reduced rate is equidistant from two one-hundredths of a cent, to
      the higher of them,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the final rate is or includes a specific rate
      expressed in cents to one decimal place, to the nearest one-tenth
      of a cent or, if the specific rate component of the reduced rate
      is equidistant from two one-tenths of a cent, to the higher of
      them, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, to the nearest cent or, if
      the specific rate component of the reduced rate is equidistant
      from two cents, to the higher of them; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is "Free" or does not include a specific rate, the
    specific rate component of the reduced rate shall be rounded as
    provided in subparagraphs (a)(i) to (iii), except that the
    references to the final rate in subparagraphs (a)(i) and (ii) shall
    be read as references to the initial rate.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension or withdrawal of entitlement

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, by
  order, amend the schedule to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} extend entitlement to the Most-Favoured-Nation
    Tariff to any goods that originate in a country to which the General
    Tariff applies;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} withdraw entitlement to the Most-Favoured-Nation
    Tariff from any goods that originate in a country that is entitled
    to that Tariff and make those goods subject to the General Tariff;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} indicate, to the extent required, the tariff
    treatment of the country to which the order applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (1) must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} specify the date on which the order becomes
    effective;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the order partially extends entitlement to the
    Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff, indicate the goods to which entitlement
    to that Tariff is extended; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the order partially withdraws entitlement to the
    Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff, indicate the goods that are made
    subject to the General Tariff.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 31
- 2011, c. 24, s. 117
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-31-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 31"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval by Parliament

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  order made under paragraph 31(1)(b) the period of which is longer than
  180 days ceases to have effect on the one hundred and eightieth day
  after the day on which it becomes effective or, if Parliament is not
  then sitting, the fifteenth day thereafter that Parliament is sitting
  unless, not later than that day, the order is approved by a resolution
  adopted by both Houses of Parliament.

- Meaning of [[sitting day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), a day on which
  either House of Parliament sits is deemed to be a sitting day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rates restored

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an order referred to in subsection (1) ceases to
  have effect under that subsection, entitlement to the
  Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff withdrawn by the order shall be restored.

#### [General Preferential Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143117 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of GPT

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 24 and 35 and
  any order made under section 34, goods that originate in a country set
  out in the List of Countries as a beneficiary of the General
  Preferential Tariff are entitled to the General Preferential Tariff
  rates of customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "GPT" in relation to goods entitled to the General
  Preferential Tariff, the General Preferential Tariff rate of customs
  duty that applies to those goods is the final rate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for GPT

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "GPT" in relation to goods entitled to the General
  Preferential Tariff, the General Preferential Tariff rate of customs
  duty that applies to those goods is the initial rate, reduced as
  provided in the "F" Staging List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"J" staging for GPT

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If "J" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "GPT" in relation to goods entitled to the General
  Preferential Tariff, the General Preferential Tariff rate of customs
  duty that applies to those goods is the initial rate, reduced by one
  percentage point on January 1 of each year after 1998, until the
  difference between the reduced rate and the final rate is less than
  one percentage point, at which time the final rate applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding amounts

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per cent other
  than 0.5, the resulting percentage shall be rounded down to the
  nearest percentage that divides evenly by 0.5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elimination of rates less than two per
  cent

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) in respect
  of goods other than motor vehicles of tariff item No. 8703.21.10 or
  8705.20.00 results in a rate of customs duty that is a percentage of
  less than two per cent, the rate shall be further reduced to "Free"
  immediately.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension and withdrawal of entitlement

- **[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, by
  order, amend the schedule to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} extend entitlement to the General Preferential
    Tariff to any goods that originate in a country that is a
    beneficiary of the Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff if, in the opinion of
    the Governor in Council, that country is a developing country;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} withdraw entitlement to the General Preferential
    Tariff from any goods that originate in a country that is a
    beneficiary of that Tariff; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} reduce a rate of customs duty set out following the
    abbreviation "GPT" in the column entitled "Preferential Tariff" in
    the List of Tariff Provisions and the "F" Staging List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must specify the date on which the order becomes
    effective;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must, if the order partially extends entitlement to
    the General Preferential Tariff, indicate the goods to which
    entitlement to that Tariff is extended;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} may exempt goods from the conditions set out in
    subsection 24(1) and prescribe any conditions that apply; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} must, if the order wholly or partially withdraws
    entitlement to the General Preferential Tariff, indicate the goods
    to which the Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff applies as a consequence.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 34
- 2011, c. 24, s. 118
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-34-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 34"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of tariff rate quota

- **[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, by
  order, apply a tariff rate quota in respect of goods imported from one
  or more countries entitled to the General Preferential Tariff for a
  period specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tariff treatment if tariff rate quota
  exceeded

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Goods imported in excess of a tariff rate quota
  applied by an order made under subsection (1) are subject to the
  tariff treatment that would be applicable to those goods if they were
  not entitled to the General Preferential Tariff.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expiry date

**[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** Sections 33 to 35 cease
to have effect on December 31, 2034 or on any earlier date that may be
fixed by order of the Governor in Council.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 36
- 2004, c. 13, s. 1
- 2013, c. 33, s. 62

  ajax-after="1409816-1414229.html"}
:::

#### [General Preferential Tariff Plus]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1414230 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of GPTP

- **[[36.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 24 and 36.3 and
  any order made under section 36.2, goods that originate in a country
  set out in the List of Countries as a beneficiary of the General
  Preferential Tariff Plus are entitled to the General Preferential
  Tariff Plus rates of customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "GPTP" in relation to goods entitled to the General
  Preferential Tariff Plus, the General Preferential Tariff Plus rate of
  customs duty that applies to those goods is the final rate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for GPTP

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "GPTP" in relation to goods entitled to the General
  Preferential Tariff Plus, the General Preferential Tariff Plus rate of
  customs duty that applies to those goods is the initial rate, reduced
  as provided in the "F" Staging List.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 232](#1409816-1414239){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1409816-1414239.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension or withdrawal of entitlement

- **[[36.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the
  recommendation of the Minister, by order, amend the schedule to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} extend entitlement to the General Preferential
    Tariff Plus to any goods that originate in a country that is a
    beneficiary of the General Preferential Tariff, if, in the opinion
    of the Governor in Council, that country conforms to international
    norms relating to sustainable development and labour and human
    rights;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} withdraw entitlement to the General Preferential
    Tariff Plus from any goods that originate in a country that is a
    beneficiary of that Tariff; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} reduce a rate of customs duty set out following the
    abbreviation "GPTP" in the column entitled "Preferential Tariff" in
    the List of Tariff Provisions and the "F" Staging List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must specify the date on which the order becomes
    effective;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must, if the order partially extends entitlement to
    the General Preferential Tariff Plus, indicate the goods to which
    that Tariff is extended;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} may exempt the goods from the conditions set out in
    subsection 24(1) and prescribe any conditions that apply; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} must, if the order wholly or partially withdraws
    entitlement to the General Preferential Tariff Plus, indicate the
    goods to which the General Preferential Tariff applies as a
    consequence.

  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the
  recommendation of the Minister, by order, apply a tariff rate quota in
  respect of goods imported from one or more countries entitled to the
  General Preferential Tariff Plus for a period specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tariff treatment if tariff rate quota

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Goods imported in excess of a tariff rate quota

#### [Least Developed Country Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143154 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of LDCT

  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 24 and 39 and
  any order made under section 38, goods that originate in a country set
  out in the List of Countries as a beneficiary of the Least Developed
  Country Tariff are entitled to the Least Developed Country Tariff
  rates of customs duty.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "LDCT" in relation to goods entitled to the Least
  Developed Country Tariff, the Least Developed Country Tariff rate of
  customs duty that applies to those goods is the final rate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for LDCT

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "LDCT" in relation to goods entitled to the Least
  Developed Country Tariff, the Least Developed Country Tariff rate of
  customs duty that applies to those goods is the initial rate, reduced
  as provided in the "F" Staging List.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension or withdrawal of entitlement

  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, by
  order, amend the schedule to

    Tariff to any goods that originate in a country that is a

    of the Governor in Council, that country is a least developed
    country;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} withdraw entitlement to the Least Developed Country
    Tariff from any goods that originate in a country that is a
    beneficiary of that Tariff; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} reduce a rate of customs duty set out following the
    abbreviation "LDCT" in the column entitled "Preferential Tariff" in
    the List of Tariff Provisions and the "F" Staging List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of order

    effective;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must, if the order partially extends entitlement to
    the Least Developed Country Tariff, indicate the goods to which that
    Tariff is extended;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} may exempt the goods from the conditions set out in

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} must, if the order wholly or partially withdraws
    entitlement to the Least Developed Country Tariff, indicate the
    goods to which the General Preferential Tariff applies as a

#### [Commonwealth Caribbean Countries Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143185 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of CCCT

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 24 and 43 and
  any order made under section 42, goods that originate in a country set
  out in the List of Countries as a beneficiary of the Commonwealth
  Caribbean Countries Tariff are entitled to the Commonwealth Caribbean
  Countries Tariff rates of customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "CCCT" in relation to goods entitled to the Commonwealth
  Caribbean Countries Tariff, the Commonwealth Caribbean Countries
  Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those goods is the final
  rate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for CCCT

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "CCCT" in relation to goods entitled to the Commonwealth
  Caribbean Countries Tariff, the Commonwealth Caribbean Countries
  Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those goods is the initial
  rate, reduced as provided in the "F" Staging List.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension or withdrawal of entitlement

- **[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The

  order, amend the schedule to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} extend entitlement to the Commonwealth Caribbean
    Countries Tariff to any goods that originate in a country that is a
    beneficiary of that Tariff;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} withdraw entitlement to the Commonwealth Caribbean
    Countries Tariff from any goods that originate in a country that is
    a beneficiary of that Tariff; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} reduce a rate of customs duty set out following the
    abbreviation "CCCT" in the column entitled "Preferential Tariff" in
    the List of Tariff Provisions and the "F" Staging List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must specify the date on which the order becomes
    effective;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must, if the order extends or wholly or partially
    withdraws entitlement to the Commonwealth Caribbean Countries
    Tariff, indicate the goods to which the order applies; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} may exempt goods from the conditions set out in
    subsection 24(1) and prescribe any conditions that apply.
