#### [Australia Tariff and New Zealand Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143211 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of AUT

- **[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that
  originate in Australia are entitled to the Australia Tariff rates of
  customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of NZT

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that originate in New
  Zealand are entitled to the New Zealand Tariff rates of customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "AUT" or "NZT" in relation to goods entitled to the
  Australia Tariff or the New Zealand Tariff, as the case may be, the
  rate of customs duty that applies to those goods under that Tariff is
  the final rate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staging for AUT and NZT

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If "B" or "E" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "AUT" or "NZT" in relation to goods entitled to the
  Australia Tariff or the New Zealand Tariff, as the case may be, the
  rate of customs duty that applies to those goods under that Tariff is
  the initial rate, reduced

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if "B" is set out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 1999, by one half of the
      difference between the initial rate and the final rate, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2000, to the final rate;
      and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if "E" is set out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 1999, by one sixth of the
      difference between the initial rate and the final rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2000, by one third of
      the difference between the initial rate and the final rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2001, by one half of
      the difference between the initial rate and the final rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2002, by two thirds of
      the difference between the initial rate and the final rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2003, by five sixths of
      the difference between the initial rate and the final rate, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2004, to the final rate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for AUT and NZT

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "AUT" or "NZT" in relation to goods entitled to the
  Australia Tariff or the New Zealand Tariff, as the case may be, the
  rate of customs duty that applies to those goods under that Tariff is
  the initial rate, reduced as provided in the "F" Staging List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding percentage rates

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (4) or (5) results in
  a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per cent, the
  resulting percentage shall be rounded to the nearest one-tenth of one
  per cent or, if the resulting percentage is equidistant from two
  one-tenths of one per cent, to the higher of them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding percentage rates

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (4) or (5) or a
  rounding of rates under subsection (6) results in a rate of customs
  duty that includes a fraction of one per cent other than 0.5, the
  resulting percentage shall be rounded down to the nearest percentage
  that divides evenly by 0.5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding specific rates

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (4) or (5) results in
  a rate of customs duty that includes a specific rate that includes a
  fraction of one cent and the final rate

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is or includes a specific rate, the specific rate
    component of the reduced rate shall be rounded

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the final rate is or includes a specific rate
      expressed in cents to two decimal places, to the nearest
      one-hundredth of a cent or, if the specific rate component of the
      reduced rate is equidistant from two one-hundredths of a cent, to
      the higher of them,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the final rate is or includes a specific rate
      expressed in cents to one decimal place, to the nearest one-tenth
      of a cent or, if the specific rate component of the reduced rate
      is equidistant from two one-tenths of a cent, to the higher of
      them, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, to the nearest cent or, if
      the specific rate component of the reduced rate is equidistant
      from two cents, to the higher of them; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is "Free" or does not include a specific rate, the
    specific rate component of the reduced rate shall be rounded as
    provided in subparagraphs (a)(i) to (iii), except that the
    references to the final rate in subparagraphs (a)(i) and (ii) shall
    be read as references to the initial rate.

#### [United States Tariff and Mexico Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143244 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of UST

- **[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that
  are entitled to the United States Tariff are entitled to the United
  States Tariff rates of customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate for UST

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "UST" in relation to goods entitled to the United States
  Tariff, the United States Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to
  those goods is the final rate of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for UST

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "UST" in relation to goods entitled to the United States
  Tariff, the United States Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to
  those goods is the initial rate, reduced as provided in the "F"
  Staging List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of MXT

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that are entitled to the
  Mexico Tariff are entitled to the Mexico Tariff rates of customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate for MXT

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "MXT" in relation to goods entitled to the Mexico Tariff,
  the rate of customs duty that applies to those goods under that Tariff
  is the final rate of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for MXT

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "MXT" in relation to goods entitled to the Mexico Tariff,
  the Mexico Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those goods is
  the initial rate, reduced as provided in the "F" Staging List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of UST and MXT

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Despite any other provision of this Act, for the
  purpose of giving effect to Annex 6-A of the Canada--United
  States--Mexico Agreement, the Minister may, by order, amend the
  schedule to extend entitlement to the United States Tariff or the
  Mexico Tariff to any imported goods under any conditions that are
  specified in the order.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s. 190\]]{.Repealed}

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s. 190\]]{.Repealed}

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s. 190\]]{.Repealed}

- [(11)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s. 190\]]{.Repealed}

- [(12)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s. 190\]]{.Repealed}

- [(13)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s. 190\]]{.Repealed}

#### [Chile Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143285 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of CT

- **[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that
  originate in Chile are entitled to the Chile Tariff rates of customs
  duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "CT" in relation to goods entitled to the Chile Tariff,
  the Chile Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those goods is
  the final rate of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for CT

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "CT" in relation to goods entitled to the Chile Tariff,
  the Chile Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those goods is
  the initial rate, reduced as provided in the "F" Staging List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staging for CT

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If "G", "K", "K1", "D1", "I", "I1" or "L" is set out
  in the column entitled "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff
  Provisions following the abbreviation "CT" in relation to goods
  entitled to the Chile Tariff, the Chile Tariff rate of customs duty
  that applies to those goods is the initial rate, reduced

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if "G" is set out, effective on January 1, 1999, to
    the final rate of "Free";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if "K" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 1999, to four fifths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2000, to three fifths of
      the initial rate, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2001, to the final rate
      of "Free";

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if "K1" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 1999, to 86% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2000, to 60% of the
      initial rate, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2001, to the final rate
      of "Free";

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if "D1" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 1999, to three quarters
      of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2000, to one half of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2001, to one quarter of
      the initial rate, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2002, to the final rate
      of "Free";

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if "I" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 1999, to four fifths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2000, to three fifths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2001, to two fifths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2002, to one fifth of
      the initial rate, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2003, to the final rate
      of "Free";

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if "I1" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 1999, to 84% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2000, to 60% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2001, to 42% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2002, to 20% of the
      initial rate, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2003, to the final rate
      of "Free"; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if "L" is set out, effective on January 1, 2003, to
    the final rate of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of specific rates

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a specific rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one tenth
  of a cent, the rate shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of
  a cent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of amounts

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per cent other
  than 0.5, the resulting percentage shall be rounded down to the
  nearest percentage that divides evenly by 0.5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elimination of rates of less than two
  per cent

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a rate of customs duty that is a percentage of less than two per cent,
  the rate shall be further reduced to "Free" immediately.

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2011, c. 24,
s. 121\]]{.Repealed}

Version](section-47-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 47"){.PITLink}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of Chile Tariff

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** Notwithstanding any other
provision of this Act and for the purpose of giving effect to Appendix
5.1 of Annex C-00-B of the Canada--Chile Free Trade Agreement, the
Minister may, by order, amend the schedule to extend entitlement to the
Chile Tariff to any imported goods under such conditions as are
specified in the order.

#### [Colombia Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143332 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of COLT

- **[[49.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that
  originate in Colombia are entitled to the Colombia Tariff rates of
  customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "COLT" in relation to goods entitled to the Colombia
  Tariff, the Colombia Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those
  goods is the final rate of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for COLT

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "COLT" in relation to goods entitled to the Colombia
  Tariff, the Colombia Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those
  goods is the initial rate, reduced as provided in the "F" Staging
  List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staging for COLT

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If "S1", "S2" or "S3" is set out in the column
  entitled "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions
  following the abbreviation "COLT" in relation to goods entitled to the
  Colombia Tariff, the Colombia Tariff rate of customs duty that applies
  to those goods is the initial rate, reduced

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if "S1" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the coming into force of this
      subsection, to two thirds of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is one
      year after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to one third of the initial rate, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is two
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to the final rate of "Free";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if "S2" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the coming into force of this
      subsection, to six sevenths of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is one
      year after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to five sevenths of the initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is two
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to four sevenths of the initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is three
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to three sevenths of the initial rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is four
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to two sevenths of the initial rate,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is five
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to one seventh of the initial rate, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is six
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to the final rate of "Free"; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if "S3" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the coming into force of this
      subsection, to sixteen seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is one
      year after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to fifteen seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is two
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to fourteen seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is three
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to thirteen seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is four
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to twelve seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is five
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to eleven seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is six
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to ten seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is
      seven years after the year of the coming into force of this
      subsection, to nine seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is eight
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to eight seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is nine
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to seven seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is 10
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to six seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is 11
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to five seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(xiii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is 12
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to four seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(xiv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is 13
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to three seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(xv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is 14
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to two seventeenths of the initial rate,

    - [(xvi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is 15
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to one seventeenth of the initial rate, and

    - [(xvii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is 16
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to the final rate of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of specific rates

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a specific rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one tenth
  of a cent, the rate shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of
  a cent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of amounts

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per cent other
  than 0.5, the resulting percentage shall be rounded down to the
  nearest percentage that divides evenly by 0.5.

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in

#### [Costa Rica Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143382 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of CRT

- **[[49.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that
  originate in Costa Rica are entitled to the Costa Rica Tariff rates of
  customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "CRT" in relation to goods entitled to the Costa Rica
  Tariff, the Costa Rica Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to
  those goods is the final rate of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for CRT

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "CRT" in relation to goods entitled to the Costa Rica
  Tariff, the Costa Rica Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to
  those goods is the initial rate, reduced as provided in the "F"
  Staging List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"M" Staging for CRT

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If "M" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "CRT" in relation to goods entitled to the Costa Rica
  Tariff, the Costa Rica Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to
  those goods is the initial rate, reduced to a final rate of "Free"
  when the Minister is satisfied that Costa Rica has eliminated all
  business income tax exemptions and other export subsidies in respect
  of goods produced wholly or partially within a geographic area, as
  defined in subsection 16(2.1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staging for CRT

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If "N", "O" or "P" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "CRT" in relation to goods entitled to the Costa Rica
  Tariff, the Costa Rica Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to
  those goods is the initial rate, reduced

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if "N" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the coming into force of this
      subsection, to four fifths of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2003, to three fifths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2004, to two fifths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2005, to one fifth of
      the initial rate, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2006, to the final rate
      of "Free";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if "O" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the coming into force of this
      subsection, to seven eighths of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2003, to six eighths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2004, to five eighths
      of the initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2005, to four eighths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2006, to three eighths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2007, to two eighths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2008, to one eighth of
      the initial rate, and

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2009, to the final
      rate of "Free"; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if "P" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2003, to eight ninths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2004, to seven ninths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2005, to six ninths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2006, to five ninths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2007, to four ninths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2008, to three ninths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2009, to two ninths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2010, to one ninth of
      the initial rate, and

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2011, to the final rate
      of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of specific rates

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (5) results in
  a specific rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one tenth
  of a cent, the rate shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of
  a cent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of amounts

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (5) results in
  a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per cent other
  than 0.5, the resulting percentage shall be rounded down to the
  nearest percentage that divides evenly by 0.5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elimination of rates of less than two
  per cent

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (5) results in
  a rate of customs duty that is a percentage of less than two per cent,
  the rate shall be further reduced to "Free" immediately.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 28, s. 37
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of Costa Rica Tariff

**[[49.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.2 .sectionLabel}** Notwithstanding any
other provision of this Act and for the purpose of giving effect to
Appendix III.1.6.1 of Annex III.1 of the Canada --- Costa Rica Free
Trade Agreement, the Minister may, by order, amend the schedule to
extend entitlement to the Costa Rica Tariff to any imported goods under

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reduction of rate: "M" Staging

**[[49.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.4 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, by order, amend the
List of Tariff Provisions and the "F" Staging List to reduce, subject to
the conditions specified in the order, the initial rate for goods of a
tariff item entitled to the Costa Rica Tariff in respect of which "M" is
set out in the column entitled "Preferential Tariff" in the List of
Tariff Provisions following the abbreviation "CRT" in that tariff item.

- 2001, c. 28, s. 37

- **[[49.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that

  duty.

  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the

  the Peru Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those goods is

  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "PT" in relation to goods entitled to the Peru Tariff,
  the Peru Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those goods is

  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "PT" in relation to goods entitled to the Peru Tariff,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2010, to five sevenths

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1, 2015, to the final rate
      of "Free".

  1702.90.61, 1702.90.70 and 1702.90.81 that are entitled to the Peru
  Tariff, and the limits apply during the periods and subject to the
  conditions that may be specified in the order.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 16, s. 42
:::

#### [Honduras Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143509 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of HNT

- **[[49.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that
  originate in Honduras are entitled to the Honduras Tariff rates of
  customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate for HNT

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "HNT" in relation to goods entitled to the Honduras
  Tariff, the Honduras Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those
  goods is the final rate of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for HNT

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "HNT" in relation to goods entitled to the Honduras
  Tariff, the Honduras Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those
  goods is the initial rate, reduced as provided in the "F" Staging
  List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staging for HNT

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If "U1" or "U2" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "HNT" in relation to goods entitled to the Honduras
  Tariff, the Honduras Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those
  goods is the initial rate, reduced

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if "U1" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the coming into force of this
      subsection, to two thirds of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is one
      year after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to one third of the initial rate, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is two
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to the final rate of "Free"; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if "U2" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the coming into force of this
      subsection, to six sevenths of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is one
      year after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to five sevenths of the initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is two
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to four sevenths of the initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is three
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to three sevenths of the initial rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is four
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to two sevenths of the initial rate,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is five
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to one seventh of the initial rate, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the year that is six
      years after the year of the coming into force of this subsection,
      to the final rate of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of specific rates

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a specific rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one tenth
  of a cent, the rate shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of
  a cent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of amounts

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per cent other
  than 0.5, the resulting percentage shall be rounded down to the
  nearest percentage that divides evenly by 0.5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elimination of rates of less than two
  per cent

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a rate of customs duty that is a percentage of less than two per cent,
  the rate shall be further reduced to "Free" immediately.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of Honduras Tariff

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding any other provision of this Act, for
  the purpose of giving effect to Annex 3.1 of the Canada--Honduras Free
  Trade Agreement, the Minister may, by order, amend the schedule to
  extend entitlement to the Honduras Tariff to any imported goods under
  such conditions as may be specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limits on reduction of duty

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of
  the Minister, by order, specify limits on the aggregate quantity of
  goods of tariff item Nos. 1701.91.10, 1701.99.10, 1702.90.21,
  1702.90.61, 1702.90.70 and 1702.90.81 that are entitled to the
  Honduras Tariff, and the limits apply during the periods and subject
  to the conditions that may be specified in the order.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 14, s. 43
:::

#### [Korea Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143543 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of KRT

- **[[49.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that
  originate in Korea are entitled to the Korea Tariff rates of customs
  duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate for KRT

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "KRT" in relation to goods entitled to the Korea Tariff,
  the Korea Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those goods is
  the final rate of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for KRT

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "KRT" in relation to goods entitled to the Korea Tariff,
  the Korea Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those goods is
  the initial rate, reduced as provided in the "F" Staging List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staging for KRT

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If "V1", "V2", "V3" or "V4" is set out in the column
  entitled "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions
  following the abbreviation "KRT" in relation to goods entitled to the
  Korea Tariff, the Korea Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to
  those goods is the initial rate, reduced

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if "V1" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the coming into force of this
      subsection, to 66.7% of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is one year after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to 33.3% of the
      initial rate, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is two years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to the final
      rate of "Free";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if "V2" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the coming into force of this
      subsection, to 80% of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is one year after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to 60% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is two years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to 40% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is three years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to 20% of the
      initial rate, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is four years after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to the final rate
      of "Free";

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if "V3" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the coming into force of this
      subsection, to 90% of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is one year after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to 80% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is two years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to 70% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is three years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to 60% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is four years after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to 50% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is five years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to 40% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is six years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to 30% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is seven years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to 20% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is eight years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to 10% of the
      initial rate, and

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is nine years after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to the final rate
      of "Free"; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if "V4" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the coming into force of this
      subsection, to 90.9% of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is one year after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to 81.8% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is two years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to 72.7% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is three years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to 63.6% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is four years after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to 54.5% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is five years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to 45.5% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is six years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to 36.4% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is seven years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to 27.3% of the
      initial rate,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is eight years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to 18.2 per
      cent of the initial rate,

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is nine years after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to 9.1% of the
      initial rate, and

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is ten years after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to the final rate
      of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of specific rates

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a specific rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one tenth
  of a cent, the rate shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of
  a cent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of amounts --- fraction other
  than 0.5

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per cent other
  than 0.5, the resulting percentage shall be rounded down to the
  nearest percentage that divides evenly by 0.5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elimination of rates less than 2%

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a rate of customs duty that is a percentage of less than two per cent,
  the rate shall be further reduced to "Free" immediately.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 28, s. 47

#### [Canada--European Union Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143594 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of CEUT

- **[[49.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that
  originate in an EU country or other CETA beneficiary are entitled to
  the Canada--European Union Tariff rates of customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate for CEUT

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "CEUT" in relation to goods entitled to the
  Canada--European Union Tariff, the Canada--European Union Tariff rate
  of customs duty that applies to those goods is the final rate of
  "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for CEUT

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "CEUT" in relation to goods entitled to the
  Canada--European Union Tariff, the Canada--European Union Tariff rate
  of customs duty that applies to those goods is the initial rate,
  reduced as provided in the "F" Staging List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staging for CEUT

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If "W1", "W2", "W3" or "W4" is set out in the column
  entitled "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions
  following the abbreviation "CEUT" in relation to goods entitled to the
  Canada--European Union Tariff, the Canada--European Union Tariff rate
  of customs duty that applies to those goods is the initial rate,
  reduced

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if "W1" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the coming into force of this
      section, to three quarters of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the first year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to one half of
      the initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the second year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to one quarter of
      the initial rate, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the third year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to the final rate
      of "Free";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if "W2" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the coming into force of this
      section, to five sixths of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the first year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to two thirds of
      the initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the second year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to one half of
      the initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the third year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to one third of
      the initial rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the fourth year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to one sixth of
      the initial rate, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the fifth year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to the final rate
      of "Free";

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if "W3" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the coming into force of this
      section, to seven eighths of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the first year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to three quarters
      of the initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the second year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to five eighths
      of the initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the third year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to one half of
      the initial rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the fourth year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to three eighths
      of the initial rate,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the fifth year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to one quarter of
      the initial rate,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the sixth year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to one eighth of
      the initial rate, and

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the seventh year
      after the year in which this section comes into force, to the
      final rate of "Free"; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if "W4" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the fifth anniversary of the day on
      which this section comes into force, to two thirds of the initial
      rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the sixth year after
      the year in which this section comes into force, to one third of
      the initial rate, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the seventh year
      after the year in which this section comes into force, to the
      final rate of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of specific rates

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a specific rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one tenth
  of a cent, the rate shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of
  a cent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of amounts

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per cent, the
  resulting percentage shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of
  one percent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of amounts --- fraction other
  than 0.5

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If, for any goods other than motor vehicles of
  heading No. 87.02, 87.03 or 87.04, a reduction under subsection (3)
  or (4) or a rounding of rates under subsection (6) results in a rate
  of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per cent other than
  0.5, the resulting percentage shall be rounded down to the nearest
  percentage that divides evenly by 0.5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elimination of rates of less than 2%

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If, for any goods other than motor vehicles of
  heading No. 87.02, 87.03 or 87.04, a reduction under subsection (3)
  or (4) results in a rate of customs duty that is a percentage of less
  than two per cent, the rate shall be further reduced to "Free"
  immediately.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2017, c. 6, s. 97
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[49.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.9 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, make regulations
defining the term "EU country or other CETA beneficiary".

::: HistoricalNote
- 2017, c. 6, s. 97
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension and withdrawal of entitlement

- **[[49.91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.91
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the
  recommendation of the Minister, by order, amend the schedule to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} extend entitlement to the Canada--European Union
    Tariff to any goods that originate in an EU country or other CETA
    beneficiary; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} withdraw entitlement to the Canada--European Union
    Tariff from any goods that originate in a country if, in the opinion
    of the Governor in Council, those goods are not entitled to that
    Tariff under the Canada--European Union Comprehensive Economic and
    Trade Agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must specify the date on which the order becomes
    effective;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must, if the order partially extends entitlement to
    the Canada--European Union Tariff, indicate the goods to which
    entitlement to that Tariff is extended;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} may exempt goods from the conditions set out in
    subsection 24(1) and prescribe any conditions that apply; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} must, if the order wholly or partially withdraws
    entitlement to the Canada--European Union Tariff, indicate the goods
    to which the Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff applies as a consequence.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2017, c. 6, s. 97
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of UKT

- **[[49.92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49.92
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that
  originate in a CUKTCA beneficiary are entitled to the United Kingdom
  Tariff rates of customs duty.

  abbreviation "UKT" in relation to goods entitled to the United Kingdom
  Tariff, the United Kingdom Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for UKT

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of CIAT

  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that
  originate in Israel or another CIFTA beneficiary are entitled to the
  Canada--Israel Agreement Tariff rates of customs duty.

#### [Iceland Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143673 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of IT

- **[[52.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that
  are entitled to the Iceland Tariff are entitled to the Iceland Tariff
  rates of customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "IT" in relation to goods entitled to the Iceland Tariff,
  the Iceland Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those goods is
  the final rate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for IT

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "IT" in relation to goods entitled to the Iceland Tariff,
  the Iceland Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those goods is
  the initial rate, reduced as provided in the "F" Staging List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staging for IT

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If "Q1" or "Q2" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "IT" in relation to goods entitled to the Iceland Tariff,
  the Iceland Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those goods is
  the initial rate, reduced

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if "Q1" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is three years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to seven
      eighths of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is four years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to six eighths
      of the initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is five years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to five eighths
      of the initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is six years after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to four eighths of
      the initial rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is seven years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to three
      eighths of the initial rate,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is eight years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to two eighths
      of the initial rate,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is nine years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to one eighth
      of the initial rate, and

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is 10 years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to the final
      rate of "Free"; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if "Q2" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is three years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to twelve
      thirteenths of the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is four years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to eleven
      thirteenths of the initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is five years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to ten
      thirteenths of the initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is six years after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to nine thirteenths
      of the initial rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is seven years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to eight
      thirteenths of the initial rate,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is eight years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to seven
      thirteenths of the initial rate,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is nine years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to six
      thirteenths of the initial rate,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is 10 years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to five
      thirteenths of the initial rate,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is 11 years after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to four thirteenths
      of the initial rate,

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is 12 years after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to three
      thirteenths of the initial rate,

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is 13 years after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to two thirteenths
      of the initial rate,

    - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is 14 years after the
      day on which this subsection comes into force, to one thirteenth
      of the initial rate, and

    - [(xiii)]{.lawlabel} effective on the day that is 15 years after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force, to the final
      rate of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of specific rates

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a specific rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one tenth
  of a cent, the rate shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of
  a cent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of amounts

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per cent other
  than 0.5, the resulting percentage shall be rounded down to the
  nearest percentage that divides evenly by 0.5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elimination of rates of less than two
  per cent

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a rate of customs duty that is a percentage of less than two per cent,
  the rate shall be further reduced to "Free" immediately.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 6, s. 33

  Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Tariff, the
  Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Tariff rate of
  customs duty that applies to those goods is the final rate of "Free".

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled

  Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Tariff, the

  customs duty that applies to those goods is the initial rate, reduced
  as provided in the "F" Staging List.

  Partnership Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those goods is

  a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per cent, the
  resulting percentage shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of

  than 0.5

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If, for any goods other than motor vehicles of
  heading No. 87.01, 87.02, 87.03, 87.04 or 87.05, a reduction under
  subsection (3) or (4) or a rounding of rates under subsection (6)
  results in a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per
  cent other than 0.5, the resulting percentage shall be rounded down to
  the nearest percentage that divides evenly by 0.5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elimination of rates of less than 2%

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If, for any goods other than motor vehicles of
  heading No. 87.01, 87.02, 87.03, 87.04 or 87.05, a reduction under
  subsection (3) or (4) results in a rate of customs duty that is a
  percentage of less than two per cent, the rate shall be further
  reduced to "Free" immediately.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 23, s. 43
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of entitlement

- **[[52.61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52.61
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the
  recommendation of the Minister, by order, amend the schedule to extend
  entitlement to the Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific
  Partnership Tariff to any goods that originate in a CPTPP country.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retroactivity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (1) may, if it so
  provides, be retroactive and have effect in respect of a period before
  it is made, but no such order may have effect in respect of a period
  before this section comes into force.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must specify the date on which the order becomes
    effective;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must, if the order partially extends entitlement to
    the Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Tariff,
    indicate the goods to which entitlement to that Tariff is extended;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} may exempt goods from the conditions set out in
    subsection 24(1) and prescribe any conditions that apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 23, s. 43
:::

#### [Comprehensive and Progressive Australia Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-143900 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of CPAUT

- **[[52.62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52.62
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 24, goods that
  are entitled to the Comprehensive and Progressive Australia Tariff are
  entitled to the Comprehensive and Progressive Australia Tariff rates
  of customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate for CPAUT

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "CPAUT" in relation to goods entitled to the
  Comprehensive and Progressive Australia Tariff, the Comprehensive and
  Progressive Australia Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to
  those goods is the final rate of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for CPAUT

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If "F" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "CPAUT" in relation to goods entitled to the
  Comprehensive and Progressive Australia Tariff, the Comprehensive and
  Progressive Australia Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to
  those goods is the initial rate, reduced as provided in the "F"
  Staging List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staging for CPAUT

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If "X8", "X9", "X10", "X11", "X12", "X13" or "X14" is
  set out in the column entitled "Preferential Tariff" in the List of
  Tariff Provisions following the abbreviation "CPAUT" in relation to
  goods entitled to the Comprehensive and Progressive Australia Tariff,
  the Comprehensive and Progressive Australia Tariff rate of customs
  duty that applies to those goods is the initial rate, reduced

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if "X8" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the first day on which the
      Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Agreement
      is in effect between Canada and Australia, to three quarters of
      the initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the first year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to one half of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the second year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to one quarter of the
      initial rate, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the third year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to the final rate of
      "Free";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if "X9" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the first day on which the
      Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Agreement
      is in effect between Canada and Australia, to five sixths of the
      initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the first year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to two thirds of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the second year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to one half of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the third year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to one third of the
      initial rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the fourth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to one sixth of the
      initial rate, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the fifth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to the final rate of
      "Free";

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if "X10" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the first day on which the
      Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Agreement
      is in effect between Canada and Australia, to six sevenths of the
      initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the first year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to five sevenths of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the second year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to four sevenths of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the third year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to three sevenths of the
      initial rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the fourth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to two sevenths of the
      initial rate,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the fifth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to one seventh of the
      initial rate, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the sixth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to the final rate of
      "Free";

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if "X11" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the first day on which the
      Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Agreement
      is in effect between Canada and Australia, to ten elevenths of the
      initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the first year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to nine elevenths of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the second year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to eight elevenths of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the third year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to seven elevenths of the
      initial rate,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the fourth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to six elevenths of the
      initial rate,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the fifth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to five elevenths of the
      initial rate,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the sixth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to four elevenths of the
      initial rate,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the seventh year
      after the year in which that first day occurs, to three elevenths
      of the initial rate,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the eighth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to two elevenths of the
      initial rate,

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the ninth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to one eleventh of the
      initial rate, and

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the tenth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to the final rate of
      "Free";

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if "X12" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the eighth year after
      the year in which the first day on which the Comprehensive and
      Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Agreement is in effect
      between Canada and Australia occurs, to three quarters of the
      initial rate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the ninth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to one half of the
      initial rate,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the tenth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to one quarter of the
      initial rate, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the eleventh year
      after the year in which that first day occurs, to the final rate
      of "Free";

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if "X13" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the first day on which the
      Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Agreement
      is in effect between Canada and Australia, to one quarter of the
      initial rate, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the eleventh year
      after the year in which that first day occurs, to the final rate
      of "Free"; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if "X14" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the first day on which the
      Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Agreement
      is in effect between Canada and Australia, to a rate of 5.5 per
      cent,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the first year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to a rate of 5.0 per
      cent,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the second year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to a rate of 2.5 per
      cent,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the third year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to a rate of 2.0 per
      cent, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the fourth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to the final rate of
      "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of specific rates

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a specific rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one tenth
  of a cent, the rate shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of
  a cent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of amounts

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per cent, the
  resulting percentage shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of
  one per cent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of amounts -- fraction other
  than 0.5

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If, for any goods other than motor vehicles of
  heading No. 87.01, 87.02, 87.03, 87.04 or 87.05, a reduction under
  subsection (3) or (4) or a rounding of rates under subsection (6)
  results in a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per
  cent other than 0.5, the resulting percentage shall be rounded down to
  the nearest percentage that divides evenly by 0.5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elimination of rates of less than 2%

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If, for any goods other than motor vehicles of
  heading No. 87.01, 87.02, 87.03, 87.04 or 87.05, a reduction under
  subsection (3) or (4) results in a rate of customs duty that is a
  percentage of less than two per cent, the rate shall be further
  reduced to "Free" immediately.

#### [Comprehensive and Progressive Chile Tariff]{.HTitleText3} {#h-144056 .Subheading}

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if "X23" is set out,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the eleventh year

      of "Free";

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if "X27" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the first day on which the
      Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Agreement
      is in effect between Canada and Chile, to one quarter of the
      initial rate, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the eleventh year

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if "X28" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the first day on which the
      Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Agreement
      is in effect between Canada and Chile, to a rate of 5.5 per cent,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the first year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to a rate of 5.0 per
      cent,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the second year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to a rate of 2.5 per
      cent,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the third year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to a rate of 2.0 per
      cent, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the fourth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to the final rate of
      "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of specific rates

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a specific rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one tenth
  of a cent, the rate shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of
  a cent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of amounts

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per cent, the
  resulting percentage shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of
  one per cent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of amounts --- fraction other
  than 0.5

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If, for any goods other than motor vehicles of
  heading No. 87.01, 87.02, 87.03, 87.04 or 87.05, a reduction under
  subsection (3) or (4) or a rounding of rates under subsection (6)
  results in a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per
  cent other than 0.5, the resulting percentage shall be rounded down to
  the nearest percentage that divides evenly by 0.5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elimination of rates of less than 2%

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If, for any goods other than motor vehicles of
  heading No. 87.01, 87.02, 87.03, 87.04 or 87.05, a reduction under
  subsection (3) or (4) results in a rate of customs duty that is a
  percentage of less than two per cent, the rate shall be further
  reduced to "Free" immediately.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 23, s. 43
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of entitlement

- **[[52.67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52.67
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the
  recommendation of the Minister, by order, amend the schedule to extend
  entitlement to the Comprehensive and Progressive Chile Tariff to any
  goods that originate in a CPTPP country.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retroactivity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (1) may, if it so
  provides, be retroactive and have effect in respect of a period before
  it is made, but no such order may have effect in respect of a period
  before the first day on which the Comprehensive and Progressive
  Trans-Pacific Partnership Agreement is in effect between Canada and
  Chile.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must specify the date on which the order becomes
    effective;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must, if the order partially extends entitlement to
    the Comprehensive and Progressive Chile Tariff, indicate the goods
    to which entitlement to that Tariff is extended; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} may exempt goods from the conditions set out in
    subsection 24(1) and prescribe any conditions that apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 23, s. 43
:::

  are entitled to the Comprehensive and Progressive Japan Tariff are
  entitled to the Comprehensive and Progressive Japan Tariff rates of
  customs duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"A" final rate for CPJPT

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If "A" is set out in the column entitled
  "Preferential Tariff" in the List of Tariff Provisions following the
  abbreviation "CPJPT" in relation to goods entitled to the
  Comprehensive and Progressive Japan Tariff, the Comprehensive and
  Progressive Japan Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those
  goods is the final rate of "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}"F" staging for CPJPT

  Comprehensive and Progressive Japan Tariff, the Comprehensive and
  Progressive Japan Tariff rate of customs duty that applies to those
  goods is the initial rate, reduced as provided in the "F" Staging
  List.

      of "Free";

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if "X41" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the first day on which the
      Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Agreement
      is in effect between Canada and Malaysia, to one quarter of the
      initial rate, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the eleventh year
      after the year in which that first day occurs, to the final rate
      of "Free"; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if "X42" is set out,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} effective on the first day on which the
      Comprehensive and Progressive Trans-Pacific Partnership Agreement
      is in effect between Canada and Malaysia, to a rate of 5.5 per
      cent,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the first year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to a rate of 5.0 per
      cent,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the second year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to a rate of 2.5 per
      cent,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the third year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to a rate of 2.0 per
      cent, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} effective on January 1 of the fourth year after
      the year in which that first day occurs, to the final rate of
      "Free".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of specific rates

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a specific rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one tenth
  of a cent, the rate shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of
  a cent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of amounts

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a reduction under subsection (3) or (4) results in
  a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per cent, the
  resulting percentage shall be rounded down to the nearest one tenth of
  one per cent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of amounts --- fraction other
  than 0.5

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If, for any goods other than motor vehicles of
  heading No. 87.01, 87.02, 87.03, 87.04 or 87.05, a reduction under
  subsection (3) or (4) or a rounding of rates under subsection (6)
  results in a rate of customs duty that includes a fraction of one per
  cent other than 0.5, the resulting percentage shall be rounded down to
  the nearest percentage that divides evenly by 0.5.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elimination of rates of less than 2%

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If, for any goods other than motor vehicles of
  heading No. 87.01, 87.02, 87.03, 87.04 or 87.05, a reduction under
  subsection (3) or (4) results in a rate of customs duty that is a
  percentage of less than two per cent, the rate shall be further
  reduced to "Free" immediately.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 23, s. 43
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of entitlement

- **[[52.71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52.71
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the
  recommendation of the Minister, by order, amend the schedule to extend
  entitlement to the Comprehensive and Progressive Malaysia Tariff to
  any goods that originate in a CPTPP country.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retroactivity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (1) may, if it so
  provides, be retroactive and have effect in respect of a period before
  it is made, but no such order may have effect in respect of a period
  before the first day on which the Comprehensive and Progressive
  Trans-Pacific Partnership Agreement is in effect between Canada and
  Malaysia.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must specify the date on which the order becomes
    effective;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must, if the order partially extends entitlement to
    the Comprehensive and Progressive Malaysia Tariff, indicate the
    goods to which entitlement to that Tariff is extended; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} may exempt goods from the conditions set out in
    subsection 24(1) and prescribe any conditions that apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 23, s. 43
:::
