[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

- **[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to regulations made under
  paragraph 99(e), the Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
  Preparedness may issue a numbered certificate to a person of a
  prescribed class referred to in section 89.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment, suspension, etc., of
  certificate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
  Preparedness may, subject to regulations made under paragraph 99(e),
  amend, suspend, renew, cancel or reinstate a certificate issued under
  subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release of goods

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Goods in respect of which relief is granted under
  section 89 may be released without payment of the duties relieved
  under that section if the number of the certificate issued under
  subsection (1) is disclosed when the goods are accounted for under
  section 32 of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and the certificate
  is in force at that time.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 90
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-90-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 90"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister may issue licence for operation
of bonded warehouse

- **[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  Minister of Public Safety and Emergency Preparedness considers it
  advisable, that Minister may issue a licence for the operation of any
  place as a bonded warehouse to a person who meets the qualifications
  prescribed by regulations made under subparagraph 99(f)(i) and any
  requirements or conditions set out in this Act, the [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and the regulations under both Acts respecting
  the operation of that place.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licence restriction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to regulations made under paragraph 99(f),
  the Minister of Public Safety and Emergency Preparedness may impose in
  a licence issued under subsection (1) any restriction as to the
  classes of goods that may be received, or the circumstances under
  which goods may be received, in the bonded warehouse.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment of licence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
  Preparedness may, subject to regulations made under paragraph 99(f),
  amend, suspend, renew, cancel or reinstate a licence issued under
  subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person to whom a licence is issued under
  subsection (1) shall, at the request of the Minister of Public Safety
  and Emergency Preparedness, provide security, of a type and in
  accordance with the conditions that may be prescribed, in an amount
  satisfactory to that Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 91
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-91-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 91"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties not payable in respect of goods in
bonded warehouse

- **[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 31 of the
  [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and to any regulations made under
  paragraph 99(f) or section 100, if a bonded warehouse licence has been
  issued under section 91, duties imposed on goods that are delivered to
  the bonded warehouse are not payable until the goods are removed from
  the bonded warehouse.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Relief on goods removed from bonded
  warehouse

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Relief shall be granted from the payment of duties
  that, but for this section, would be payable in respect of goods
  removed from a bonded warehouse that are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 95, exported directly from the
    bonded warehouse; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} designated as ships' stores by regulations made
    under paragraph 99(g), supplied for use on board a conveyance of a
    class prescribed by regulations under that paragraph and exported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply to any duty imposed under
  the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) in respect of manufactured
  tobacco that is manufactured in Canada and imported manufactured
  tobacco that is stamped in accordance with that Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 92
- 2001, c. 16, s. 5
- 2002, c. 22, s. 350
- 2008, c. 28, s. 71
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-92-20030701.html "Link to previous version of section 92"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission of evidence

**[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** If relief has been
granted under section 89 or 92, the Minister of Public Safety and
Emergency Preparedness may require the submission of evidence
satisfactory to that Minister for the purpose of administering section
95.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 93
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-93-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 93"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[customs
duties]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  sections 95, 96, 98.1 and 98.2, [[customs duties]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  means customs duties imposed under Part 2, other than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} additional customs duties levied under sections
    21.1 to 21.3; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} surtaxes or temporary duties imposed under Division
    4 of that Part.

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2011, c. 24, s. 131\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}For greater certainty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, in sections 95, 96, 98.1 and
  98.2, [[customs duties]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} does not include any
  duties or taxes levied or imposed on imported goods under the [Excise
  Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the [Special Import Measures
  Act](/eng/acts/S-15), the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) or the
  [Select Luxury Items Tax Act](/eng/acts/S-8.35).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 94
- 2001, c. 28, s. 42
- 2002, c. 19, ss. 9, 21, c. 22, s. 351
- 2011, c. 24, s. 131
- 2017, c. 6, s. 101

  ajax-after="1288398-1291346.html"}
- [2022, c. 10, s. 161](#1356825-1374208){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1356825-1374208.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-94-20210401.html "Link to previous version of section 94"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Repayment of relief

- **[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  relief is granted under section 89 or 92 in respect of goods that are
  subsequently exported to the United States or Mexico,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person who exported the goods shall, within 60
    days after exporting the goods, report the exportation in the
    prescribed manner to an officer at a customs office and pay the
    portion of the duties relieved under that section that are customs
    duties; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsections (4) to (6), notwithstanding
    any other provision of this Part, the person who exported the goods
    and any other person who was granted the relief are, from the time
    of exporting the goods, jointly and severally or solidarily liable
    to pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada the portion of the duties
    relieved under that section that are customs duties.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt to Her Majesty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An amount referred to in subsection (1), while it
  remains unpaid, is deemed to be a debt owing to Her Majesty in right
  of Canada under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2020, c. 1, s. 198\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reduction of amount repayable

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The amount of the customs duties levied under
  subsection (1) shall be reduced in accordance with subsection (5) if,
  within 60 days after the goods are exported, evidence satisfactory to
  the Minister of Public Safety and Emergency Preparedness is submitted
  to that Minister that customs duties in respect of the exportation of
  the goods have been paid to the government of the United States or of
  Mexico.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of reduction

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), the amount of customs
  duties levied under subsection (1) shall be reduced by the amount of
  customs duties paid to the government of the United States or of
  Mexico or, if that amount is equal to or greater than the amount of
  the customs duties levied, the amount levied shall be reduced to zero.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) and sections 96 to 98 do not apply in
  respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} imported goods that originate in the United States
    or Mexico that are

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsequently exported to the United States or
      Mexico,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} used as materials in the production of goods
      that are subsequently exported to the United States or Mexico, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} substituted by identical or similar goods used
      as materials in the production of other goods that are
      subsequently exported to the United States or Mexico;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} imported orange or grapefruit concentrates used in
    the manufacture or production of orange or grapefruit products of
    heading No. 20.09 that are exported to the United States;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} imported goods used as materials to make apparel
    that is exported to the United States and subject to the
    Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff in accordance with the laws of that
    country, or imported goods substituted by identical or similar goods
    used as materials to make such apparel;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} imported goods used as materials in the production
    of, or for which identical or similar goods are substituted and used
    as materials in the production of, quilted cotton piece goods and
    quilted man-made piece goods provided for under subheading 5811.00
    and furniture moving pads provided for under subheading 6307.90,
    that are exported to the United States and subject to the
    Most-Favoured-Nation Tariff in accordance with the laws of that
    country;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} imported goods that are subsequently exported in
    the condition in which they were imported;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} imported goods referred to in subsection 89(1) that
    are deemed to have been exported by reason of their having been

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} placed in a duty free shop for exportation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} designated as ships' stores by regulations made
      under paragraph 99(g),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} supplied for use on board a conveyance of a
      class prescribed by regulations made under paragraph 99(g), or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} used or destined for use, in such other manner
      as may be prescribed, solely and exclusively in conjunction with a
      project undertaken jointly by the Government of Canada and the
      government of the United States or of Mexico, or with a project in
      Canada undertaken by the government of the United States or of
      Mexico and destined to become the property of the government of
      the United States or of Mexico; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} such other imported goods or any imported goods
    used as materials, or any class of such goods, as may, on the
    recommendation of the Minister, be prescribed by the Governor in
    Council on the basis of an agreement between the Government of
    Canada and the government of the United States or of Mexico relating
    to the application of this subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions of [[identical or similar
  goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} and [[used]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[identical or similar
  goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and [[used]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} have the
  same meanings as in paragraph 7 of Article 2.5 of the Canada--United
  States--Mexico Agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[materials]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[materials]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  means goods that are used in the processing of other goods, and
  includes parts or ingredients.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 95
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145

  ajax-after="1238343-1253415.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum drawback

**[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96 .sectionLabel}** Subject to subsection
95(6), a drawback, granted under section 113, of customs duties paid in
respect of imported goods that are or were exported to the United States
or Mexico, may not exceed the lesser of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount of customs duties paid or owed in respect
  of the imported goods at the time of importation, and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount of customs duties paid to the country that
  is a party to the Canada--United States--Mexico Agreement to which the
  imported goods were subsequently exported.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 96
- [2020, c. 1, s. 199](#1238360-1253427){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1238360-1253427.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-96-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 96"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No relief or drawback of SIMA duties

**[[97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97 .sectionLabel}** Subject to subsection
95(6), relief may not be granted under section 89 or 92 and a drawback
may not be granted under section 113 of duties paid under the [Special
Import Measures Act](/eng/acts/S-15) on imported goods that are or were
exported to the United States or Mexico.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 97
- [2020, c. 1, s. 199](#1238360-1253420){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1238360-1253420.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-97-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 97"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exports to United States or Mexico

- **[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 95(6), if
  relief or a drawback of duties levied under the [Special Import
  Measures Act](/eng/acts/S-15) has been granted in respect of imported
  goods and the goods are or were exported to the United States or
  Mexico and the relief or drawback could not be granted because of
  section 97,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person who exported the goods shall, within 60
    days after exporting the goods, report the exportation in the
    prescribed manner to an officer at a customs office and pay the
    amount of the duties levied under that Act that were relieved or on
    which a drawback was granted; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} notwithstanding any other provision of this Part,
    any person who exported the goods or who was granted the relief or
    drawback is, from the time of exporting the goods, jointly and
    severally or solidarily liable to pay to Her Majesty in right of
    Canada the amount of the duties levied under that Act that were
    relieved or on which a drawback was granted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt to Her Majesty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An amount referred to in subsection (1), while it
  remains unpaid, is deemed to be a debt owing to Her Majesty in right
  of Canada under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 98
- [2020, c. 1, s. 200](#1238362-1253432){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1238362-1253432.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-98-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 98"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Repayment of relief --- EU

- **[[98.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  relief is granted under section 89 in respect of goods used as
  materials, or for which identical, equivalent or similar substitutes
  are used as materials, in the production of other goods that are
  exported, on or after the third anniversary of the day on which
  section 49.8 comes into force, to an EU country or other CETA
  beneficiary and that benefit, when imported into that country, from a
  preferential tariff under the Canada--European Union Comprehensive
  Economic Trade Agreement,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person who exported the other goods shall,
    within 60 days after exporting them, report their exportation in the
    prescribed manner to an officer at a customs office and pay an
    amount equal to the portion of the duties relieved under section 89
    that are customs duties; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (4) but despite any other
    provision of this Part, the person and any other person who was
    granted that relief are, from the time the other goods are exported,
    jointly and severally or solidarily liable to pay to Her Majesty in
    right of Canada the amount referred to in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt to Her Majesty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} While it remains unpaid, the amount referred to in
  subsection (1) is a debt owing to Her Majesty in right of Canada under
  the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No refund or drawback

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No refund or drawback may be granted under section
  113 for customs duties paid in respect of goods for which relief of
  all or a portion of the duties could have been, but was not, granted
  under section 89, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the goods are used as materials, or identical,
    equivalent or similar substitutes for those goods are used as
    materials, in the production of other goods; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} those other goods are exported, on or after the
    third anniversary of the day on which section 49.8 comes into force,
    to an EU country or other CETA beneficiary and benefit, when
    imported into that country, from a preferential tariff under the
    Canada--European Union Comprehensive Economic Trade Agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (3) do not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} imported goods that originate in an EU country or
    other CETA beneficiary that are used as materials, or for which
    identical, equivalent or similar substitutes are used as materials,
    in the production of other goods that are exported to an EU country
    or other CETA beneficiary;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} imported goods referred to in subsection 89(1) that
    are deemed to have been exported by reason of their having been

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} placed in a duty free shop for exportation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} designated as ships' stores by regulations made
      under paragraph 99(g), or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} supplied for use on board a conveyance of a
      class prescribed under paragraph 99(g); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other imported goods or any imported goods used
    as materials, or any class of such goods, that may, on the
    recommendation of the Minister, be prescribed by the Governor in
    Council on the basis of an agreement between the Government of
    Canada and the other parties to the Canada--European Union
    Comprehensive Economic and Trade Agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[materials]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[materials]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  means goods that are used in the processing of other goods, and
  includes parts or ingredients.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2017, c. 6, s. 102
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Repayment of relief --- U.K.

- **[[98.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  relief is granted under section 89 in respect of goods used as
  materials, or for which identical, equivalent or similar substitutes
  are used as materials, in the production of other goods that are
  exported to a CUKTCA beneficiary and that benefit, when imported into
  that country, from a preferential tariff under the Canada--United
  Kingdom Trade Continuity Agreement,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person who exported the other goods shall,
    within 60 days after exporting them, report their exportation in the
    prescribed manner to an officer at a customs office and pay an
    amount equal to the portion of the duties relieved under section 89
    that are customs duties; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (4) but despite any other
    provision of this Part, the person and any other person who was
    granted that relief are, from the time the other goods are exported,
    jointly and severally or solidarily liable to pay to Her Majesty in
    right of Canada the amount referred to in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt to Her Majesty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} While it remains unpaid, the amount referred to in
  subsection (1) is a debt owing to Her Majesty in right of Canada under
  the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No refund or drawback

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No refund or drawback may be granted under section
  113 for customs duties paid in respect of goods for which relief of
  all or a portion of the duties could have been, but was not, granted
  under section 89, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the goods are used as materials, or identical,
    equivalent or similar substitutes for those goods are used as
    materials, in the production of other goods; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} those other goods are exported to a CUKTCA
    beneficiary and benefit, when imported into that country, from a
    preferential tariff under the Canada--United Kingdom Trade
    Continuity Agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (3) do not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} imported goods that originate in a CUKTCA
    beneficiary that are used as materials, or for which identical,
    equivalent or similar substitutes are used as materials, in the
    production of other goods that are exported to a CUKTCA beneficiary;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} imported goods referred to in subsection 89(1) that
    are deemed to have been exported by reason of their having been

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} placed in a duty free shop for exportation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} designated as ships' stores by regulations made
      under paragraph 99(g), or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} supplied for use on board a conveyance of a
      class prescribed under paragraph 99(g); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other imported goods or any imported goods used
    as materials, or any class of such goods, that may, on the
    recommendation of the Minister, be prescribed by the Governor in
    Council on the basis of an agreement between the Government of
    Canada and the Government of the United Kingdom.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[materials]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[materials]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  means goods that are used in the processing of other goods, and
  includes parts or ingredients.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2021, c. 1, s. 42](#1288403-1291369){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1288403-1291369.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-99 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may, on the recommendation of the Minister of Public Safety and
Emergency Preparedness, make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, for the purposes of section 89,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} classes of persons who may apply for relief,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} classes of goods for which, and the circumstances
    and conditions under which, relief may not be granted,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances in which, and the classes of
    goods in respect of which, relief of duties levied under sections
    21.1 to 21.3 or under the [Special Import Measures
    Act](/eng/acts/S-15), a surtax or temporary duty imposed under
    Division 4 of Part 2, a tax levied under the [Excise Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/E-15) or a duty imposed under the [Excise Act,
    2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) may not be granted,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the period after the release of the goods within
    which those goods or the goods processed in Canada must be exported,
    and

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the portion of duties otherwise payable that may be
    relieved;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, for the purposes of paragraph 89(1)(a),
  the uses to which goods may be put or operations that goods may
  undergo after which the goods are considered to be in the same
  condition;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, for the purposes of paragraphs 89(1)(d)
  and (e), goods that are to be considered to be of the same class;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of paragraph 89(3)(b), classes of
    ships or aircraft, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of paragraph 89(3)(c), classes of
    telegraph cable ships;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, for the purposes of section 90, the
  circumstances and conditions in which a certificate may be issued,
  amended, suspended, renewed, cancelled or reinstated;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 91,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} prescribing qualifications that must be met by an
    operator of a bonded warehouse,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the conditions under which a licence
    for the operation of a bonded warehouse may be issued, including the
    security that may be required of an operator of a bonded warehouse
    in order to be granted a licence, the duration of such a licence and
    the fees or the manner of determining any fees to be paid for such a
    licence,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the form, nature and conditions of
    any required security,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the circumstances under which a
    licence for the operation of a bonded warehouse may be amended,
    suspended, renewed, cancelled or reinstated,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} establishing standards for the operation and
    maintenance of the facilities of a bonded warehouse,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner of acknowledging receipt of
    goods in a bonded warehouse,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} prescribing facilities, equipment and personnel
    that must be provided at a bonded warehouse,

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} regulating the transfer of ownership of goods in
    a bonded warehouse,

  - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} prescribing restrictions as to the classes of
    goods that may be received in a bonded warehouse,

  - [(x)]{.lawlabel} prescribing circumstances in which goods shall not
    be received in a bonded warehouse,

  - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the period within which goods that
    have been delivered to a bonded warehouse shall be removed,

  - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} prescribing classes of goods that may be
    forfeited if they are not removed from a bonded warehouse within the
    prescribed period, and

  - [(xiii)]{.lawlabel} otherwise regulating the operation of a bonded
    warehouse;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} designating certain classes of goods as ships' stores
  for use on board a conveyance of a prescribed class, including a class
  based on

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the physical attributes, functions or legal
    descriptions of conveyances,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} areas within which conveyances voyage,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} requirements, or limitations, related to voyages
    of conveyances, or

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any combination of the bases mentioned in
    subparagraphs (i) to (iii);

- [(g.1)]{.lawlabel} limiting the quantity of goods referred to in
  paragraph (g) that may be used as described in that paragraph during
  any prescribed period or periods;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} regulating or prohibiting the delivery to conveyances
  of goods designated as ships' stores;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} regulating or prohibiting the transfer from one
  conveyance to another of goods designated as ships' stores; and

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything required to be prescribed by the
  Governor in Council under sections 89 to 94 and 96 to 98.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 99
- 2001, c. 28, s. 43
- 2002, c. 19, ss. 10, 23, c. 22, ss. 352, 424
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
- 2011, c. 24, s. 132
:::

Version](section-99-20051212.html "Link to previous version of section 99"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may, on the recommendation of the Minister and the Minister of Public
Safety and Emergency Preparedness, make regulations prescribing the
circumstances under which and the extent to which goods may be
manipulated, unpacked, packed, altered or combined with other goods
while in a bonded warehouse.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 100
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

[Previous
Version](section-100-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 100"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Canadian Goods Abroad]{.HTitleText3} {#h-145706 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Relief for Canadian goods abroad

- **[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 104, if an
  application is made in accordance with section 102, relief shall be
  granted in accordance with section 105 from payment of the portion of
  the duties that, but for this section, would be payable in respect of
  goods returned to Canada within one year or such other time as may be
  prescribed after their exportation in the prescribed manner if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the goods were repaired outside Canada after being
    exported for the declared purpose of being repaired;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} equipment was added to the goods outside Canada; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the goods were the product of Canada and work was
    done outside Canada on the goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Emergency repairs

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 104, if an application is made in
  accordance with section 102, relief shall be granted from the payment
  of the whole of the duties that, but for this section, would be
  payable in respect of aircraft, vehicles or vessels returned to Canada
  after their exportation if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the aircraft, vehicles or vessels were repaired
    outside Canada as a result of an unforeseen contingency that
    occurred outside Canada; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the repairs were necessary to ensure the safe
    return to Canada of the aircraft, vehicles or vessels.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the recommendation of the Minister, prescribing
    the manner for determining what goods are considered to be a product
    of Canada for the purposes of subsection (1); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on the recommendation of the Minister of Public
    Safety and Emergency Preparedness, defining the words
    [[aircraft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[vehicles]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
    and [[vessels]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} for the purposes of subsection
    (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 101
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-101-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 101"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[102]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102 .sectionLabel}** An application for
relief under section 101 must be

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} accompanied by evidence satisfactory to the Minister
  of Public Safety and Emergency Preparedness that the goods were
  exported and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in respect of goods referred to in paragraph
    101(1)(a), repairs could not have been made in Canada at the place
    the goods were located before their exportation or within a
    reasonable distance of that place,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of equipment referred to in paragraph
    101(1)(b), the equipment added could not practicably have been added
    in Canada, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of goods referred to in paragraph
    101(1)(c), it would not have been practicable to do the work in
    Canada; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the application is made under subsection 101(2),
  made in the prescribed form and manner, with the prescribed
  information, at the time of the return to Canada of the goods in
  respect of which the application is made.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 102
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 89(F), 142, 145.
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-102-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 102"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release of returned goods

**[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 104,
goods in respect of which relief is granted under section 101 before
they are released may be released without any payment of duties.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for relief

**[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** Relief shall be granted
under section 101 in respect of goods that were returned to Canada after
being exported only if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} relief, conditional on the exportation of the goods,
  was not granted in respect of any duties paid or payable; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if an application for relief under subsection 101(1)
  is made, the portion of the duties, calculated in accordance with
  paragraph 105(1)(b), has been paid.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Value for duty of work abroad

- **[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 101(1), the portion of the duties in
  respect of which relief is granted under that subsection shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount that, but for that subsection, would be
    payable in respect of the returned goods

  less

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount obtained by applying the rate that
    would, but for that subsection, be applied to determine the duties
    under paragraph (a) to the value of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in respect of goods referred to in paragraph
      101(1)(a), the repairs made outside Canada,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of equipment referred to in paragraph
      101(1)(b), the equipment added and related work done outside
      Canada, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of goods referred to in paragraph
      101(1)(c), the work done outside Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the Governor in
  Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister of Public Safety
  and Emergency Preparedness, make regulations prescribing the method of
  determining the value of repairs made, equipment added and work done
  outside Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 105
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-105-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 105"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temporary relief of certain duties and
taxes

- **[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an application for relief is made in the prescribed circumstances by a
  person of a prescribed class and in the prescribed form and manner,
  accompanied by prescribed documents and by security of a prescribed
  nature in an amount fixed by the Minister of Public Safety and
  Emergency Preparedness, relief shall be granted from the payment of
  the whole or the prescribed portion, as the case may be, of any duty
  imposed under sections 21.1 to 21.3 or the [Excise Act,
  2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) or of any excise taxes that, but for this
  section, would be payable in respect of prescribed goods that are
  imported and subsequently exported after being used in Canada only for
  a prescribed purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release of goods

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If relief is granted under subsection (1), the goods
  may be released without the payment of the relieved duty, duties or
  taxes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Relief under subsection (1) is subject to prescribed
  conditions and to the importer of the goods establishing to the
  satisfaction of the Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
  Preparedness that the goods are exported within one year after they
  are released or, if a period is prescribed, within the prescribed
  period after they are released.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
  Preparedness may, in respect of prescribed goods, extend the period of
  exportation by not more than six months if that Minister is satisfied
  that it is not practical or possible to export the goods within the
  period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiver

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
  Preparedness may waive the requirement under subsection (1) to provide
  security.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 106
- 2002, c. 22, s. 353
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-106-20030701.html "Link to previous version of section 106"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#h-145764 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of relief

- **[[107]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 95, 98.1 and
  98.2, if relief is granted under section 89, 92, 101 or 106 from the
  payment of the whole or a portion of duties,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} no duties are payable, if the relief was from the
    payment of the whole; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the portion is not payable, if the relief was only
    from the payment of the portion.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of relief on duty paid value

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), the amount of the
  customs duties payable on goods shall, for the purposes of determining
  the duty paid value of the goods, be determined as if relief had not
  been granted under section 89, 92, 101 or 106.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of relief on value under [Excise
  Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), the amount of customs
  duties payable on goods shall, for the purposes of determining the
  value of the goods under section 215 of the [Excise Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/E-15), be determined as if relief had been granted
  under section 101 but had not been granted under section 89, 92 or
  106.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 107
- 2017, c. 6, s. 103
- [2021, c. 1, s. 43](#1288405-1291371){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1288405-1291371.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-107-20170921.html "Link to previous version of section 107"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund or cancellation of security

**[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108 .sectionLabel}** The Minister of Public
Safety and Emergency Preparedness shall refund or cancel any security
given

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the issuance of a licence under section
  91, when the licence is cancelled;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of goods that would have been classified
  under tariff item No. 9993.00.00 if they had met the conditions set
  out in that tariff item, when the goods are accounted for under
  section 32 of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and all duties
  payable in respect of the goods are paid;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of goods of tariff item No. 9993.00.00,
  when the goods are destroyed in the manner that the Minister of Public
  Safety and Emergency Preparedness directs or the destruction is
  certified by a customs officer or another person designated by the
  President of the Canada Border Services Agency;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in respect of goods of any tariff item in respect of
  which security is required, other than goods of tariff item No.
  9993.00.00, when the goods are exported in the manner and within the
  period referred to in the tariff item in respect of which security was
  given or within the period established or extended by the regulations;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in respect of goods of tariff item No. 9993.00.00,
  when the goods are exported, destroyed, consumed or expended in the
  manner and within the period referred to in that tariff item or within
  the period established or extended by the regulations; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an application for relief under section
  106, if

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the goods in respect of which the application was
    made are not exported within the period referred to in subsection
    106(3) or (4), as the case may be, the goods are accounted for under
    section 32 of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), and all duties
    and taxes payable in respect of the goods are paid,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the goods in respect of which the application was
    made are destroyed in the manner that the Minister of Public Safety
    and Emergency Preparedness directs, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the goods in respect of which the application was
    made are exported within the period referred to in subsection 106(3)
    or (4), as the case may be.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 108
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 88, 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-108-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 108"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Obsolete or Surplus Goods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-145789 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[obsolete or surplus
goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109 .sectionLabel}** In this Division,
[[obsolete or surplus goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means goods that are

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} found to be obsolete or surplus

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of imported goods, by their importer or
    owner, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, by their manufacturer, producer
    or owner;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} not used in Canada;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} destroyed in such manner as the Minister of Public
  Safety and Emergency Preparedness may direct; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} not damaged before their destruction.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 109
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-109-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 109"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Relief for obsolete or surplus goods

**[[110]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-110 .sectionLabel}** If an application is
made in accordance with section 111, a refund shall be granted of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} all duties, other than the goods and services tax,
  paid in respect of imported obsolete or surplus goods;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} all duties, other than taxes imposed under the
  [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15), paid in respect of imported goods
  processed in Canada, if the goods that result from the processing
  become obsolete or surplus goods; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} all duties, other than taxes imposed under the
  [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15), paid in respect of imported goods,
  other than fuel or plant equipment, that are directly consumed or
  expended in the processing in Canada of goods that become obsolete or
  surplus goods.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111 .sectionLabel}** An application under
section 110 must be

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} made in the prescribed form and manner, with the
  prescribed information,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the obsolete or surplus goods were imported, by
    the importer or owner of those goods, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, by the manufacturer, producer
    or owner of the obsolete or surplus goods;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} accompanied by a waiver referred to in section 119,
  if applicable, and by the prescribed documents; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} made within five years, or such other time as may be
  prescribed, after the goods in respect of which it is made are
  released.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 111
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 89(F), 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-111-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 111"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[112]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may, on the recommendation of the Minister of Public Safety and
Emergency Preparedness, make regulations prescribing documents that must
accompany an application under section 110 and the period within which
such an application must be made.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 112
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-112-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 112"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Additional Relief]{.HTitleText2} {#h-145818 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund or drawback

- **[[113]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), sections
  96, 98.1 and 98.2 and any regulations made under subsection (4), a
  refund or drawback shall be granted of all or a portion of duties if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} relief or a refund of all or a portion of the
    duties could have been, but was not, granted under section 89 or
    101;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all or a portion of the duties was paid; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an application is made in accordance with
    subsection (3) and section 119.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No refund

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No refund or drawback of the duties imposed on
  tobacco products or vaping products under the [Excise Act,
  2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) shall be granted under subsection (1), except
  if a refund of the whole or the portion of the duties is required to
  be granted under Division 3.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), an application
  must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be supported by such evidence as the Minister of
    Public Safety and Emergency Preparedness may require;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be made by a prescribed person or by a person
    belonging to a prescribed class of persons;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be made in the prescribed form and manner, with the
    prescribed information, within four years, or within such other time
    as may be prescribed, after the goods in respect of which it is made
    are released; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the goods have not been exported or deemed
    exported for the purposes of relief under section 89, disclose the
    number of the certificate issued under section 90.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, the Governor in
  Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister of Public Safety
  and Emergency Preparedness, make regulations prescribing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances in which, and the classes of
    goods in respect of which, a refund or drawback of duties levied
    under sections 21.1 to 21.3 or under the [Special Import Measures
    Act](/eng/acts/S-15), a surtax or temporary duty imposed under
    Division 4 of Part 2, a tax levied under the [Excise Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/E-15) or a duty levied under the [Excise Act,
    2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) may not be granted under subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the portion of duties paid that may be granted as a
    refund or drawback under subsection (1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the persons or classes of persons who may make an
    application for a refund or drawback under subsection (1);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the uses to which goods may be put or operations
    that goods may undergo after which the goods will be considered to
    be in the same condition;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} goods that are considered to be of the same class;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the time within which an application for a refund
    or drawback must be made;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances in which an application for a
    refund or drawback may be made;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} restrictions as to the classes of goods for which a
    refund or drawback may be granted; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances in which a refund or drawback may
    not be granted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designated goods

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Despite the exception in subsection 89(2), a refund
  or drawback of duties or taxes levied or imposed under sections 21.1
  to 21.3, the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) or the [Excise Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/E-15) shall be granted under paragraph (1)(a) on
  designated goods.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 113
- 2001, c. 28, s. 44
- 2002, c. 19, ss. 11, 25, c. 22, s. 354
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 89(F), 142, 145
- 2011, c. 24, s. 133
- 2017, c. 6, s. 104
- [2021, c. 1, s. 44](#1288407-1291373){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1288407-1291373.html"}
- [2022, c. 10, s. 96](#1356510-1376949){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1356510-1376949.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-113-20210401.html "Link to previous version of section 113"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overpayment of refund or drawback

- **[[114]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-114 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  refund or drawback is granted under section 110 or 113 to a person who
  is not eligible for the refund or drawback or in an amount exceeding
  the amount for which the person is eligible, that person shall pay to
  Her Majesty in right of Canada, on the day that the refund or drawback
  is received,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any amount for which the person is not eligible;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any interest granted under section 127 on the
    amount referred to in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt to Her Majesty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An amount referred to in subsection (1), while it
  remains unpaid, is deemed to be a debt owing to Her Majesty in right
  of Canada under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discretionary relief

- **[[115]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-115 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister or the
  Minister of Public Safety and Emergency Preparedness, by order, remit
  duties.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Scope of relief

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A remission under subsection (1) may be conditional
  or unconditional, may be granted in respect of the whole or any
  portion of the duties and may be granted regardless of whether any
  liability to pay the duties has arisen.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remission by way of refund

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If duties have been paid, a remission under
  subsection (1) shall be made by granting a refund of the duties to be
  remitted.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 115
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-115-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 115"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-145864 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debts due the Crown

**[[116]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-116 .sectionLabel}** Relief under section 89
or 101 may be refused if, at the time the relief is authorized or
required to be granted, the person to whom the relief is to be granted
is indebted to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} Her Majesty in right of Canada; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} Her Majesty in right of a province in respect of tax
  payable to the province, if there is an agreement between the
  Government of Canada and the government of the province authorizing
  Canada to collect the tax on behalf of the province.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sum in lieu of drawback, refund, etc.

**[[117]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-117 .sectionLabel}** If circumstances exist
that render it difficult to determine the exact amount of relief under
section 89, of a refund under section 110 or of a refund or drawback
under section 113 or the exact amount of a general remission of duties
under an order made under section 115 of this Act or section 23 of the
[Financial Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11), the Minister of Public
Safety and Emergency Preparedness may, with the consent of the applicant
for the relief, refund, drawback or remission, grant to the applicant a
sum, in an amount determined by that Minister, in lieu of the relief,
refund, drawback or remission.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 117
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-117-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 117"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to comply with conditions

- **[[118]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-118 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  relief from, or remission of, duties is granted under this Act, other
  than under section 92, or if remission of duties is granted under
  section 23 of the [Financial Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11) and a
  condition to which the relief or remission is subject is not complied
  with, the person who did not comply with the condition shall, within
  90 days or such other period as may be prescribed after the day of the
  failure to comply,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} report the failure to comply to an officer at a
    customs office; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada an amount
    equal to the amount of the duties in respect of which the relief or
    remission was granted, unless that person can provide evidence
    satisfactory to the Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
    Preparedness that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} at the time of the failure to comply with the
      condition, a refund or drawback would otherwise have been granted
      if duties had been paid, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the goods in respect of which the relief or
      remission was granted qualify in some other manner for relief or
      remission under this Act or the [Financial Administration
      Act](/eng/acts/F-11).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Diversions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a drawback has been granted of duties paid in
  respect of imported goods by reason of a deemed exportation under
  subsection 89(3), the goods are not subsequently exported and the
  goods are diverted to a use other than a use set out in that
  subsection, the person who diverted the goods shall, within 90 days
  after the day of the diversion,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} report the diversion to an officer at a customs
    office; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pay the amount of the drawback and the amount of
    any interest granted on the drawback under section 127.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt to Her Majesty

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An amount referred to in paragraph (1)(b) or (2)(b),
  while it remains unpaid, is deemed to be a debt owing to Her Majesty
  in right of Canada under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the recommendation of the Minister of Public
    Safety and Emergency Preparedness, prescribing time limits for the
    application of subsection (1) and the goods or classes of goods in
    respect of which, or the circumstances in which, those limits apply;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on the recommendation of the Minister, prescribing
    the circumstances in which certain goods are exempted from the
    application of subsection (1) and the goods or classes of goods in
    respect of which, and the period for which and the conditions under
    which, those exemptions apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 118
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-118-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 118"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waivers

**[[119]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-119 .sectionLabel}** An application under
section 110 or 113 must be accompanied by a waiver, in the prescribed
form, from every other person eligible to claim a drawback, refund or
remission of the duties in respect of which the application is made,
waiving that person's right to apply for the drawback, refund or
remission.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 119
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 89(F), 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-119-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 119"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[value]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[120]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
sections 121 and 122, [[value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of
a by-product, goods or merchantable scrap or waste,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the processor has sold the by-product, goods or
  merchantable scrap or waste in an arms-length transaction, the price
  at which the processor sold the by-product, goods or merchantable
  scrap or waste; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the price at which the processor
  would ordinarily have sold the by-product, goods or merchantable scrap
  or waste in an arms-length transaction,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application for a drawback or
    refund, at the time the application is made, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if relief from the payment of duties has been
    granted under section 89, at the time the goods are exported.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Relief reduced by value of by-product

- **[[121]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-121 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  relief from payment of duties is granted in respect of goods under
  section 89 and the goods enter into a process that produces a
  by-product in respect of which relief could not have been granted, the
  processor shall, within 90 days after production of the by-product,
  pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada an amount that bears the same
  proportion to the amount of the relief as the value of the by-product
  bears to the total value of the products produced from the processing
  of the goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt to Her Majesty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An amount referred to in subsection (1), while it
  remains unpaid, is deemed to be a debt owing to Her Majesty in right
  of Canada under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of drawback or refund not paid
  reduced by value of by-product

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If goods in respect of which an application was made
  under section 110 or 113 enter into a process that produces a
  by-product in respect of which a refund or drawback cannot be granted
  and the drawback or refund has not yet been paid, the amount of the
  drawback or refund shall be reduced by the same proportion that the
  value of the by-product bears to the total value of the products
  produced from the processing of the goods.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Relief reduced by value of merchantable
scrap or waste

- **[[122]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  goods in respect of which relief is granted under section 89 enter
  into a process that produces merchantable scrap or waste in respect of
  which the relief could not have been granted, the processor shall,
  within 90 days after the production of the scrap or waste, pay to Her
  Majesty in right of Canada an amount equal to the product obtained by
  multiplying the value of the merchantable scrap or waste by the rate
  of customs duty that applies, at the time that the scrap or waste was
  produced, to merchantable scrap or waste of the same kind.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt to Her Majesty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An amount referred to in subsection (1), while it
  remains unpaid, is deemed to be a debt owing to Her Majesty in right
  of Canada under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Drawback or refund reduced by value of
  merchantable scrap or waste

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If goods in respect of which an application was made
  under section 110 or 113 enter into a process that produces
  merchantable scrap or waste in respect of which a refund or drawback
  cannot be granted and the drawback or refund has not yet been paid,
  the amount of the drawback or refund shall be reduced by an amount
  equal to the product obtained by multiplying the value of the
  merchantable scrap or waste by the rate of customs duty that applies,
  at the time the merchantable scrap or waste results from the process,
  to merchantable scrap or waste of the same kind.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest on overpayment in relation to a
refund or drawback

- **[[123]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person who is liable under subsection 114(1) to pay an amount, other
  than an amount in respect of duty levied under the [Special Import
  Measures Act](/eng/acts/S-15), shall pay, in addition to the amount,
  interest at the specified rate for the period beginning on the first
  day after the refund or drawback was granted and ending on the day the
  amount is paid in full, calculated on the amount of the balance
  outstanding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest on failure to comply or
  diversion

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), a person who is liable
  under subsection 118(1) or (2) to pay an amount, other than an amount
  in respect of duty levied under the [Special Import Measures
  Act](/eng/acts/S-15), shall pay, in addition to the amount, interest
  at the specified rate for the period beginning on the day that the
  liability was incurred and ending on the day the amount is paid in
  full, calculated on the amount of the balance outstanding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest on by-products and
  merchantable scrap or waste

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), a person who is liable
  under section 121 or 122 to pay an amount, other than an amount in
  respect of duty levied under the [Special Import Measures
  Act](/eng/acts/S-15), shall pay, in addition to the amount, interest
  at the specified rate for the period beginning on the first day after
  the production of the by-product or merchantable scrap or waste and
  ending on the day the amount is paid in full, calculated on the amount
  of the balance outstanding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a person pays an amount owing under paragraph
  118(1)(b) or section 121 or 122 within the 90 day period referred to
  in that paragraph or section, no interest on the amount is payable by
  the person under subsection (2) or (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Computation of interest on certain
  duties

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person who is liable under paragraph 118(1)(b) or
  section 121 or 122 to pay an amount in respect of duty levied under
  the [Special Import Measures Act](/eng/acts/S-15) shall pay interest
  at the specified rate for the period beginning on the ninety-first day
  after the day the amount became payable and ending on the day the
  amount is paid in full, calculated on the amount of the balance
  outstanding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Computation of interest on certain
  duties

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A person who is liable under section 98, subsection
  114(1) or paragraph 118(2)(b) to repay the amount of a drawback or
  relief in respect of duty levied under the [Special Import Measures
  Act](/eng/acts/S-15) and any interest on the drawback shall pay, in
  addition to those amounts, interest at the specified rate for the
  period beginning on the first day after the day the drawback was
  granted or the person failed to comply with a condition to which the
  relief was subject, as the case may be, and ending on the day those
  amounts are repaid in full, calculated on the amount of the balance
  outstanding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest on the payment of relief for
  NAFTA

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A person who is liable under subsection 95(1) to pay
  an amount, other than an amount in respect of duty levied under the
  [Special Import Measures Act](/eng/acts/S-15), shall pay, in addition
  to the amount, interest at the specified rate for the period beginning
  on the sixty-first day after the day the amount became payable and
  ending on the day the amount is paid in full, calculated on the amount
  of the balance outstanding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest on the payment of relief for
  CETA

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A person who is liable under subsection 98.1(1) to
  pay an amount shall pay, in addition to the amount, interest at the
  specified rate for the period beginning on the sixty-first day after
  on which the day the amount became payable and ending on the day on
  which the amount is paid in full, calculated on the amount of the
  balance outstanding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest on the payment of relief for
  CUKTCA

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A person who is liable under subsection 98.2(1) to
  pay an amount shall pay, in addition to the amount, interest at the
  specified rate for the period beginning on the sixty-first day after
  the day on which the amount became payable and ending on the day on
  which the amount is paid in full, calculated on the amount of the
  balance outstanding.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 123
- 2001, c. 25, s. 88
- 2017, c. 6, s. 105
- [2021, c. 1, s. 45](#1288409-1291376){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1288409-1291376.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-123-20170921.html "Link to previous version of section 123"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest to be compounded

**[[124]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124 .sectionLabel}** Interest computed at a
prescribed rate or at a specified rate shall be compounded daily and, if
interest computed in respect of an amount under a provision of this Act
is unpaid on the day it would, but for this section, have ceased to be
computed under that provision, interest at the specified rate, computed
and compounded daily on the unpaid interest from that day to the day it
is paid, shall be paid in the same manner as the provision requires the
principal amount to be paid.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 124
- 2001, c. 25, s. 89
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed rate may be authorized

**[[125]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-125 .sectionLabel}** The Minister of Public
Safety and Emergency Preparedness may authorize persons who are required
under a provision of this Act to pay interest on an amount at a
specified rate to instead pay interest under that provision at the
prescribed rate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 125
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-125-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 125"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiver of interest

- **[[126]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-126 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister of Public Safety and Emergency Preparedness may at any time
  waive or cancel payment of all or any portion of any interest
  otherwise payable under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest on interest refunded

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, as a result of a waiver or cancellation under
  subsection (1), a person is refunded an amount of interest that was
  paid, the person shall be given, in addition to the refund, interest
  at the prescribed rate for the period beginning on the first day after
  the day the amount was paid and ending on the day the refund is given,
  calculated on the amount of the refund.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 126
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-126-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 126"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest

- **[[127]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-127 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who is granted a refund or drawback of duties under section 110
  or 113, other than duty levied under the [Special Import Measures
  Act](/eng/acts/S-15), shall be granted, in addition to the drawback or
  refund, interest on the drawback or refund at the prescribed rate for
  the period beginning on the ninety-first day after an application for
  the drawback or refund is made in accordance with this Part and ending
  on the day the drawback or refund is granted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest on SIMA duty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who, under a provision of this Part other
  than section 115, is granted a drawback or refund of an amount in
  respect of duty levied under the [Special Import Measures
  Act](/eng/acts/S-15) shall be granted, in addition to the drawback or
  refund, interest on it at the prescribed rate for the period beginning
  on the ninety-first day after the day an application for the drawback
  or refund is made in accordance with this Part and ending on the day
  the drawback or refund is granted.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 127
- 2001, c. 25, s. 90
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment out of C.R.F.

**[[128]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-128 .sectionLabel}** A drawback or refund
granted under this Part shall be paid out of the Consolidated Revenue
Fund.

## [PART 4]{.HLabel1}[Regulations and Orders]{.HTitleText1} {#h-145959 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[129]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-129 .sectionLabel}** The Minister of Public
Safety and Emergency Preparedness may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of tariff item No. 9813.00.00 or
  9814.00.00, permitting the importation free of customs duties of
  containers not originating in Canada, if that Minister is satisfied
  that a like quantity of usable containers has been exported; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of tariff item No. 9897.00.00,
  prescribing

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} conditions under which specimens of aigrettes,
    egret plumes or osprey plumes and the feathers, quills, heads,
    wings, tails, skins or parts of skins of wild birds of that tariff
    item may be imported for any museum or for scientific or educational
    purposes, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which materials from used or
    second-hand mattresses shall be cleaned and fumigated and the
    certificates that shall accompany those materials.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 129
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-129-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 129"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of the Minister of Public Safety
and Emergency Preparedness

**[[130]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-130 .sectionLabel}** The Minister of Public
Safety and Emergency Preparedness may

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} specify documentation that is considered acceptable
  for the purposes of tariff item No. 9827.00.00; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} recognize authorities, representatives or authorized
  persons in a country of origin as competent for the purposes of
  conditions of classification of goods under a tariff item.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 130
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 142, 145
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-130-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 130"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power of the Minister

**[[131]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-131 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
designate goods for the purposes of tariff item No. 9938.00.00.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[132]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, make
  regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} amending the List of Tariff Provisions to change or
    prescribe conditions of classification of goods for the purposes of
    Chapter 99 of that List;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing territories for the purposes of the
    definition [[country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 2(1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing eligible Commonwealth countries or
    conditions for eligibility of Commonwealth countries for the
    purposes of a tariff item of heading No. 51.11, 51.12 or 58.03;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing a rate of interest or rules for
    determining a rate of interest for the purposes of any provision of
    this Act;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} reducing the maximum value of goods that are
    entitled to be classified under a tariff item of heading No. 98.04;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of tariff item No. 9808.00.00,
    withdrawing privileges from persons or classes of persons who are
    referred to in that tariff item and are from a country that refuses
    to grant the same privileges to Canadian officials holding
    corresponding or equivalent posts in that country;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of tariff item No. 9810.00.00,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} designating institutions, foreign countries and
      military service agencies, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} withdrawing privileges from persons or classes
      of persons who are referred to in that tariff item and are from a
      country that refuses to grant corresponding privileges;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} amending the list of products set out in tariff
    item No. 9905.00.00;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} amending the list of goods in tariff item No.
    9987.00.00;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} in respect of goods or classes of goods of heading
    No. 98.26, amending the schedule to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} add, delete or amend tariff items relating to
      goods or classes of goods classified under each tariff item of
      that heading,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} amend the rates of customs duty levied on goods
      or classes of goods classified under a tariff item of that
      heading,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} amend the conditions under which goods or
      classes of goods may be imported under a tariff item of that
      heading,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} exclude any goods or classes of goods from the
      application of a tariff item of that heading,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} define terms of that heading, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} amend the maximum value of goods that may be
      imported under a tariff item of that heading;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} reducing any customs duty imposed on goods of
    Chapter 89 in the List of Tariff Provisions in such circumstances
    and under such conditions as are prescribed;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of tariff item No. 9993.00.00,
    limiting or restricting the use, kinds or quantity of goods that may
    be classified under that tariff item;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of tariff item No. 9897.00.00,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} amending that tariff item to exclude goods
      manufactured or produced wholly or in part by prison labour from
      that tariff item, or prescribing the conditions under which such
      goods may be excluded from that tariff item,

    - [(i.1)]{.lawlabel} amending that tariff item to exclude goods that
      are mined, manufactured or produced wholly or in part by [[forced
      labour]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} or [[child
      labour]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as those terms are defined in
      section 2 of the [Fighting Against Forced Labour and Child Labour
      in Supply Chains Act](/eng/acts/F-10.6), from that tariff item, or
      prescribing the conditions under which such goods may be excluded
      from that tariff item,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} amending that tariff item to exclude used or
      second-hand motor vehicles manufactured before the calendar year
      in which importation is sought to be made from that tariff item,
      or prescribing the conditions under which such vehicles may be
      excluded from that tariff item, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} amending that tariff item to exclude used or
      second-hand aircraft from that tariff item, or prescribing the
      conditions under which such aircraft may be excluded from that
      tariff item;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of tariff item No. 9898.00.00,
    amending that tariff item to prescribe conditions under which arms,
    military stores, munitions of war or offensive weapons are excluded
    from that tariff item;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that is to be prescribed under
    a tariff item in Chapter 98 or 99 in the List of Tariff Provisions;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that may be prescribed under
    this Act; and

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} generally, for carrying out the purposes and
    provisions of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval by Parliament

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Regulations made under paragraph (1)(e), or under
  subparagraph (1)(j)(vi) that reduce the maximum value of goods, cease
  to have effect on the one hundred and eightieth day after the day on
  which they become effective or, if Parliament is not then sitting, the
  fifteenth day thereafter that either House of Parliament is sitting
  unless, not later than that day, the regulations are approved by a
  resolution adopted by both Houses of Parliament.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of [[sitting
  day]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), a day on which
  either House of Parliament sits is deemed to be a sitting day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum value restored

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} At the time regulations referred to in subsection (2)
  cease to have effect, the maximum value shall be restored.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retroactive effect

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A regulation made under paragraph (1)(d) that
  provides that it is to come into force on a day earlier than the day
  on which it is registered under section 6 of the [Statutory
  Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) comes into force on that earlier day
  if it gives effect to a public announcement made on or before that
  earlier day.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 132

  ajax-after="1398087-1437391.html"}
:::
