[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[133]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-133 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may, on the recommendation of the Minister of Public Safety and
Emergency Preparedness, make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, for the purposes of section 101,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the period after the exportation of goods within
    which the goods must be returned to Canada, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} what constitutes satisfactory evidence of
    exportation of the goods;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} defining the expressions
  [[baggage]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef},
  [[conveyance]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[former
  resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[incidental to the international
  traffic of the goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef},
  [[resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef},
  [[temporarily]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} and [[temporary
  resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} for the purposes of a tariff item of
  Chapter 98 of the List of Tariff Provisions;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of tariff item No. 9801.10.10,
  9801.10.20, 9801.10.30, 9801.20.00, 9808.00.00 or 9810.00.00,
  prescribing conditions under which goods may be imported;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of tariff item No. 9802.00.00,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} prescribing conditions under which conveyances may
    be imported,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} limiting the length of time that any imported
    conveyance may remain in Canada and the use that may be made of the
    conveyance while it remains in Canada, and authorizing the Minister
    of Public Safety and Emergency Preparedness to extend those limits,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} excluding any class of conveyance from
    classification under that tariff item, and

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} authorizing the Minister of Public Safety and
    Emergency Preparedness to require security for imported conveyances
    and limit the amount and type of security that may be required;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of tariff item No. 9803.00.00,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} prescribing conditions under which goods or
    conveyances may be imported and authorizing the Minister of Public
    Safety and Emergency Preparedness to establish such conditions in
    specified circumstances,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} limiting the quantity of any class of goods that
    may be imported and authorizing the Minister of Public Safety and
    Emergency Preparedness to increase those limits in specified
    circumstances,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} limiting the length of time that imported goods
    or conveyances may remain in Canada and authorizing the Minister of
    Public Safety and Emergency Preparedness to extend those limits,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} excluding any class of goods or conveyances from
    classification under that tariff item, and

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} authorizing the Minister of Public Safety and
    Emergency Preparedness to require security for imported goods or
    conveyances and limit the amount and type of security that may be
    required;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of heading No. 98.04 or of tariff
  item No. 9807.00.00, 9813.00.00, 9814.00.00, 9816.00.00, 9938.00.00 or
  9989.00.00, prescribing conditions under which goods may be imported;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of tariff item No. 9805.00.00,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} exempting goods or classes of goods imported by any
    class of persons referred to in that tariff item from any of its
    requirements relating to the period during which goods must be
    owned, possessed or used abroad, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} substituting less exigent requirements relating to
    the period during which goods or classes of goods of that tariff
    item must be owned, possessed or used abroad by any class of persons
    referred to in that tariff item;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of tariff item No. 9807.00.00,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} defining the word
    [[settler]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef},

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} exempting goods or classes of goods imported by
    any classes of persons referred to in that tariff item from any of
    its requirements relating to ownership, possession or use, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} substituting less exigent requirements relating
    to the ownership, possession or use of goods or classes of goods of
    that tariff item;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of tariff item No. 9897.00.00,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} defining the expressions
    [[issue]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef},
    [[periodical]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} and [[special
    edition]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef},

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribing conditions under which an issue of a
    periodical will be found to be an issue of a special edition that
    contained an advertisement that was primarily directed to a market
    in Canada and that did not appear in identical form in all editions
    of that issue of a periodical that were distributed in the country
    of origin, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} prescribing conditions under which an issue of a
    periodical will be found to be an issue more than five per cent of
    the advertising space in which consisted of space used for
    advertisements that indicated specific sources of availability in
    Canada, or specific conditions relating to the sale or provision in
    Canada, of any goods or services;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of tariff item No. 9971.00.00,
  prescribing conditions under which goods that have been exported to

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other orders

- **[[134]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-134 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister of Public Safety and Emergency Preparedness or President of
  the Canada Border Services Agency may, by order, suspend for a
  specified period a tariff item referred to in Supplementary Note 2(c)
  of Chapter 7 of the List of Tariff Provisions, and bring into force
  for the period one or more tariff items referred to in Supplementary
  Note 2(b) of that Chapter, in respect of goods that are imported
  through a customs office in a region or part of Canada specified in
  the order during that period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other orders

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
  Preparedness or President of the Canada Border Services Agency may, by
  order, suspend for a specified period a tariff item referred to in
  Supplementary Note 3(c) of Chapter 8 of the List of Tariff Provisions,
  and bring into force for the period one or more tariff items referred
  to in Supplementary Note 3(b) of that Chapter, in respect of goods
  that are imported through a customs office in a region or part of
  Canada specified in the order during that period.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 134
- 1999, c. 17, s. 131
- 2005, c. 38, ss. 141, 142, 145
- 2018, c. 27, s. 78
:::

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exempt goods

- **[[135]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-135 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  order made under subsection 134(1) or (2) does not apply to goods that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} before the order comes into force, a person
    purchased for importation through a customs office in a region or
    part of Canada specified in the order in the expectation in good
    faith that the "Free" rate of customs duty set out in a tariff item
    suspended by the order would apply to the goods; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the time the order comes into force, were in
    transit to the purchaser in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exempt from SIA

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order referred to in subsection 134(1) or (2) is
  deemed not to be a regulation within the meaning of the [Statutory
  Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

## [PART 5]{.HLabel1}[Prohibited Goods]{.HTitleText1} {#h-146096 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited imports

- **[[136]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-136 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  importation of goods of tariff item No. 9897.00.00, 9898.00.00 or
  9899.00.00 is prohibited.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsection 10(1) does not apply

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 10(1) does not apply in respect of goods
  referred to in subsection (1).

## [PART 6]{.HLabel1}[Transitional Provisions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-146102 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[former
Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[137]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-137 .sectionLabel}** In sections 140 and 143
to 146, [[former Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Customs
Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) as it read immediately before the coming
into force of section 214.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 137
- 2011, c. 24, s. 135
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-137-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 137"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[138]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2011, c.
24, s. 135\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-138-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 138"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[139]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-139 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2011, c.
24, s. 135\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-139-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 139"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}References to former tariff items and
codes

- **[[140]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-140
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), if a
  tariff item or code or portion of a tariff item or code of the former
  Act is referred to in an Act of Parliament, or in a regulation or
  order made thereunder, the reference to that tariff item, code or
  portion shall, unless the context requires otherwise, be construed as
  a reference to the tariff item or portion of a tariff item of this Act
  referring to goods that correspond most closely to goods referred to
  in the tariff item, code or portion of a tariff item or code of the
  former Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A reference in a provision of an Act of Parliament
  other than this Act, or of an order or regulation made under an Act of
  Parliament, to a heading, subheading, tariff item or code, or portion
  of a heading, subheading, tariff item or code, of the former Act or to
  a note to a chapter of Schedule I to the former Act shall, for any
  purpose relating to a duty or tax under the [Excise
  Act](/eng/acts/E-14) or the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) or for
  any purpose relating to an additional duty under section 21 of this
  Act, be read as a reference to that heading, subheading, tariff item,
  code, portion or note as it read immediately before the day on which
  this section comes into force.

**[[141]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-141 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2011, c.
24, s. 136\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-141-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 141"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[142]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-142 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2011, c.
24, s. 136\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-142-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 142"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuation of regulations and orders

**[[143]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-143 .sectionLabel}** If goods were accounted
for under section 32 of the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) before the
day on which this section comes into force and were subject to the
former Act, the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) or any other Act of
Parliament, or to any regulation or order made thereunder, those Acts,
regulations or orders continue to apply to those goods after the coming
into force of this section.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bonded warehouse licences continued

**[[144]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144 .sectionLabel}** Licences issued under
section 81 of the former Act and in effect on the day on which section
91 of this Act comes into force continue to have effect under that
section on and after that day.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security

**[[145]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-145 .sectionLabel}** Security held by the
Minister of National Revenue to secure the payment of duties under
subsection 81(4) of the former Act is continued as security for the
purposes of subsection 91(4) of this Act on and after the day on which
this section comes into force.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificates continued

**[[146]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-146 .sectionLabel}** Certificates issued
under section 80.1 of the former Act and in effect on the day on which
section 90 of this Act comes into force continue to have effect under
that section on and after that day.

## [PART 9]{.HLabel1}[Repeal and Coming into Force]{.HTitleText1} {#h-146128 .Part}

### [Repeal]{.HTitleText2} {#h-146129 .Subheading}

**[[213]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-213 .sectionLabel}** \[Repeal\]

### [Coming into Force]{.HTitleText2} {#h-146131 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coming into force

**[[214]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-214 .sectionLabel}** This Act comes into
force or is deemed to have come into force on January 1, 1998 and
applies, or is deemed to have applied, to all goods referred to in this
Act imported on or after that day and to goods imported before that day
that were not accounted for under section 32 of the [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) before that day.

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- 2002, c. 22, ss. 305 to 308

  - Meaning of [[implementation date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    [305]{.lawlabel} In sections 306 to 320, [[implementation
    date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the day on which Parts 3 and 4 come
    into force.

- --- 2002, c. 22, ss. 305 to 308

  - ###### Transitional treatment of duties on packaged spirits {#transitional-treatment-of-duties-on-packaged-spirits .MarginalNote}

    [306]{.lawlabel} The following rules apply to packaged spirits on
    which a duty, at a rate determined by the application of section 1
    of Part I of the schedule to the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14), was
    imposed under that Act or levied under the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) but that had not become payable before
    the implementation date:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the duty is relieved;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14)
      ceases to apply in respect of the spirits;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of imported packaged spirits that
      have not been released under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6),
      this Act, the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) apply in respect of them as though
      they were imported on that day; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other packaged spirits, this
      Act applies in respect of them as though

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} they were produced and packaged in Canada on
        that day by the person having possession of them immediately
        before that day and the person were permitted under this Act to
        produce and package them, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the spirits are in the possession of a duty
        free shop or an accredited representative or delivered as ships'
        stores in accordance with the [Ships' Stores
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40), they had been entered
        into an excise warehouse and then removed from the warehouse in
        accordance with paragraph 147(1)(a) on that day.

- --- 2002, c. 22, ss. 305 to 308

  - ###### Transitional treatment of duties on bulk spirits {#transitional-treatment-of-duties-on-bulk-spirits .MarginalNote}

    - [307]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following rules apply to
      bulk spirits on which a duty, at a rate determined by the
      application of section 1 of Part I of the schedule to the [Excise
      Act](/eng/acts/E-14), was imposed under that Act or levied under
      the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) but that had not become
      payable before the implementation date:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the duty is relieved;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the [Excise
        Act](/eng/acts/E-14) ceases to apply in respect of the spirits;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of imported bulk spirits that have
        not been released under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6),
        this Act, the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and the [Customs
        Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) apply in respect of them as though
        they were imported on that day; and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other bulk spirits, this Act
        applies in respect of them as though they were produced in
        Canada on that day by the person having possession of them
        immediately before that day.

    - ###### Transitional treatment of bulk spirits imported for bottling or blending {#transitional-treatment-of-bulk-spirits-imported-for-bottling-or-blending .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following rules apply to bulk spirits on
      which a duty, at a rate determined by the application of section 1
      of Part I of the schedule to the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14), was
      levied under the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) and remitted
      under the [Distilled Spirits for Bottling in Bond Remission
      Order](/eng/regulations/SOR-97-321) or the [Imported Spirits for
      Blending Remission Order](/eng/regulations/SI-83-151) before the
      implementation date:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the duty imposed on the spirits
        under subsection 135(1) of the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14) when
        they were entered into a distillery is relieved;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the [Excise
        Act](/eng/acts/E-14) ceases to apply in respect of the spirits;
        and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} this Act applies in respect of them as though
        they were produced in Canada on that day by the person having
        possession of them immediately before that day.

- --- 2002, c. 22, ss. 305 to 308

  - ###### Transitional treatment of excise taxes on wine {#transitional-treatment-of-excise-taxes-on-wine .MarginalNote}

    [308]{.lawlabel} The following rules apply to wine on which tax was
    imposed under section 27 of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) but
    had not become payable before the implementation date:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, the tax is relieved;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as of that day, Parts III, VI and VII of the
      [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) cease to apply in respect of the
      wine;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of imported wine that has not been
      released under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), this Act, the
      [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) apply in respect of the wine as though
      it were imported on that day;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of bulk wine to which paragraph (c)
      does not apply, this Act applies in respect of it as though it
      were produced in Canada on that day

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the wine is located in a ferment-on-premises
        facility or at the residence of an individual, by the individual
        who owned the wine immediately before that day, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, by the person having
        possession of it immediately before that day; and

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of wine to which neither
      paragraph (c) nor (d) apply, this Act applies in respect of it as
      though

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it were produced and packaged in Canada on that
        day by the person having possession of it immediately before
        that day and the person were permitted under this Act to produce
        and package it, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of wine in the possession of a
        duty free shop or an accredited representative or delivered as
        ships' stores in accordance with the [Ships' Stores
        Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40), it had been entered
        into an excise warehouse and then removed from the warehouse in
        accordance with paragraph 147(1)(a) on that day.

- --- 2002, c. 22, s. 315

  - ###### Removal of alcohol from customs bonded warehouse {#removal-of-alcohol-from-customs-bonded-warehouse .MarginalNote}

    - [315]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} If packaged alcohol is located
      in a customs bonded warehouse on the implementation date,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the alcohol shall be removed from the
        warehouse; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any duty on the alcohol that is imposed under
        this Act or levied under section 21.2 of the [Customs
        Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) as a result of the operation of
        section 306 or 308 is payable on that day unless the alcohol is
        without delay entered into an excise warehouse.

    - ###### Exception {#exception .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the alcohol in
      the customs bonded warehouse is to be

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} exported in accordance with this Act; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} delivered

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to an accredited representative for their
          personal or official use,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to a duty free shop for sale in accordance
          with the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6),

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} as ships' stores in accordance with the
          [Ships' Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40), or

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} to an air carrier that is licensed under
          section 69 or 73 of the [Canada Transportation
          Act](/eng/acts/C-10.4) to operate an international air
          service.

- --- 2002, c. 22, s. 317

  - ###### Transitional treatment of imported tobacco products {#transitional-treatment-of-imported-tobacco-products .MarginalNote}

    [317]{.lawlabel} The following rules apply to an imported tobacco
    product:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if duty levied under section 21 of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) and tax imposed under section 23 of
      the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) on the product had not become
      payable before the implementation date,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the duty and tax are relieved, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} this Act and the [Customs
        Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) apply in respect of the product as though
        it were imported into Canada on that day by the importer;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the product was stamped or marked under the
      [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14), the product is deemed to be stamped
      or marked, as the case may be, under this Act; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14) and Parts III,
      VI and VII of the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15) cease to apply
      in respect of the product.

- --- 2008, c. 28, s. 70(2)

  - - [70]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of applying the
      provisions of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) and of the
      [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) that provide for the payment of,
      or the liability to pay, interest in respect of any amount, the
      amount shall be determined and interest shall be computed on it as
      though this section had come into force on February 27, 2008.
:::

  - [235]{.lawlabel} The List of Countries and Applicable Tariff
    Treatments set out in the schedule to the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) is amended by deleting the symbol "X" in
    the column under the heading "MFN" opposite "Belarus" and "Russia"
    in the column under the heading "Country Name".
